# 8 GATES 3D — build plan and progress

**TOP PRIORITY: mobile first.** Every room, minigame panel, dialogue box, camera shot and HUD is checked at phone size (390x844 portrait, 844x390 landscape) before it counts as done.

Update this file at the end of every session. One chat per step keeps each session focused.

## ★ THE STANDARD GAME HUD (Ben, 3 Oct — locked; use on EVERY map: Meru Town Square, Arena, Bridge, Space Dock, Tank Range, all future worlds)
Reference build: `Combat Workshop.dc.html` (+ move-lab.js / vehicle-lab.js). Copy its HUD, do not redesign it. If a vehicle, sport minigame or shop needs something different, ASK Ben first and offer options.
- TOP BANNER: black, yellow rule. HP/EN (always the SELECTED fox) · CR · WEAPONS (scope icon) · MAP · CAMERA (video icon) · ☰; quest tip box (tap = JOURNAL → QUESTS, shows the SET COURSE step); time block (clock + AM/PM, date line JANUARY 1 ↔ SPEED TIME, tap = +1 hour). One shared word clock so only one top button shows a word at a time.
- COMPASS BAR just under the banner (see COMPASS BAR below); tap = minimap mode.
- BOTTOM PAD: fixed joystick with N/E/S/W arrows · EYE (hold-drag look, snaps back; tap POV) · ITEM BAG (tap = ITEMS, hold 1 s = pinned item) · FOX SWAP badge (BEN → HOPE → NOBLE) · 1 MELEE / 2 RANGED / 3 JUMP + middle MENU/SCOPE button; 70 s word loop on the pad buttons.
- MENU: tabs SCOPE (when available) · ITEMS · WEAPONS (fox: BEN/HOPE/NOBLE picker) · JOURNAL · MAP, centred white labels, HP/EN + red ✕ under the tabs; JOURNAL top row BACK TO ORBIT · OPTIONS · HOW TO · LOAD · SAVE, CHARACTER (fox cards + attributes + reputation + karma) / QUESTS (SET / UNSET COURSE); MAP with PLAY WITH MINIMAP; every close = white ✕ on red.
- CAMERA set-and-lock sheet; SCOPE called-shots overlay; PLAY WITH MINIMAP split view; phone camera (further out, leads ahead).
- ★ SHARED HUD FILE (session 26): the whole standard HUD now lives in ONE file, `Game HUD.dc.html` (banner, compass, camera sheet, toast, prompt stack, dialogue box with ✕/NEXT, key strip, phone pad, desktop round row, menu ITEMS/WEAPONS/JOURNAL/MAP). Maps mount it: `<dc-import name="Game HUD" game="{{ engine }}" hud="{{ hud }}" started busy on-hud world quest-steps orbit-url>` inside a `data-hud-host` div; it reports {pad, menu, split, bar} back through onHud so the map places its 3D stage under the banner. Change the HUD once → every map changes. Meru Town Square uses it (backup: backup/meru/Meru Town Square pre-sharedhud.dc.html). Map-only overlays (darts, jukebox, lock pick, fight panel, minigame panel, intro) stay in the map file.
  ENGINE CONTRACT every map module must expose for the HUD: start, talk, choose(i), closeDialog, nextLine, clearToast, melee, range, jump, useItem(id), closeWheel, skipTime(h), setPaused, setHudPad, setStick(x,y), eyeLook/eyeRelease/togglePov, lookBy/zoomBy/getCam/setCam, mapData() {p,b,f,e,q}, setMinimap(canvas), toggleSound, cycleWeather; onState hud {hp,hpMax,en,gold,hour,day,items,quest,place,prompt,dialog{name,role,text,choices,required},toast,weather,muted}.

  ★ VEHICLES RULE (Ben, session 28): EVERY vehicle and vehicle minigame uses the STANDARD controls + the Meru layout (Game HUD). The contract lives in vehicle-lab.js ONCE, so every ride gets it: melee/range/jump = one press of 1/2/3 (jump holds 3 for 0.9 s on tank/truck/moto = turbo burst, else 0.12 s), eyeLook/eyeRelease, togglePov = look back, getCam/setCam (zoom/pOff), mapData + setMinimap (draws course.map(): l lamps, b boards, t tokens, e enemies), setStick/setPaused already there. With opts.lock the digit keys 1/2/3 also fire (desktop holds still work: hold 1 = salvo). A course module adds map() for its minimap.
  ★ PHONE BOTTOM STRIP = THE MOBILE STANDARD (Ben, session 28; EVERY map: Town Square, Arena, Spacedock, Tank Range, all future maps + vehicles). Lives in Game HUD, nothing to add per map. On phones the old FOX SWAP badge and BAG circle are gone; ONE thin black strip runs along the very bottom (2px yellow top rule, 42px + safe area): FOX SWAP bottom-left (name in fox colour + 3 dots, tap = next fox), ITEM bottom-right ("ITEM ·" yellow + pinned item name/count WHITE; nothing pinned = "PIN AN ITEM +" → tap opens ITEMS, pick one and the menu closes; pinned: TAP = use, HOLD 0.6 s = change). Game HUD lifts its own root by the strip height while it shows, so joystick / EYE / 1·2·3 sit just above it; hidden while a menu, dialogue or busy panel is open. Vehicle maps add vehicle="<key>" (TANK weapons tab, vehicle item notes, vehicle cargo use) and hide their own stat boxes/buttons on phones (Tank Range: stats card, BOARD, UNSTUCK). Desktop keeps its bottom-left round-button row. Cycling button words still TO DISCUSS.
  ★ ITEMS STANDARD (Ben, session 28):
   - STARTING ITEMS = the 2D Meru kit: ER to GO Health Pills ×1, Energy Fill Pod ×1, Box of Chocolates ×1 (engine/save.js STARTER; old saves get it once via flag starterKit). Every map shows the same save items.
   - In a vehicle: heal items fix the vehicle (ER to GO +20 armour, Field Splint +60, Meru Amber +15 armour +1 rocket), Energy Fill Pod = full reload, Chocolates = "save it for on foot". Each vehicle page maps this in its useItem.
   - VEHICLE CARGO items (Game HUD VEH_ITEMS): items only a vehicle can use. First one: REPAIR KIT (+40 vehicle armour) for tank / boat / bike / every ride. Picked up on the course map (Tank Range: the green wrench pickups now give +1 Repair Kit, no respawn, instead of an instant heal).
   - Shown ONLY when the player has a vehicle that uses it (hud.vehicles) and ONLY under a "SEE MORE · VEHICLE CARGO (n)" button at the bottom of ITEMS. Usable (USE, pinnable) only while in that vehicle; elsewhere it says "Use it from a vehicle".
   - SKATEBOARD and MOUNTAIN BIKE are used in Town Square too: when they are in the player's SHIP CARGO, their cargo items show under SEE MORE in town as well (hud.vehicles = vehicles carried).
   - LATER (define with Ben): SHIP CARGO, BANK CARGO, HOME PLANET CARGO (where vehicles + spare items are stored, what travels with you).

   SHARED CHANGES: Game HUD VEH_SHORT/VEH_WEAPONS/IC(carve)/VEH_NOTE_BY for surf (CARVE · TRICK · JUMP). Tank Mobile Preview: Surf portrait + landscape at the top.
   ZONE: jidda-plan zones.surf (origin = the break, local -z = beach, +z = sea; note the plan's comment says +z = beach, the maths says -z). Island = jSurfContest at local (0,-140): beach, 6-tier BIG BLEACHERS (x -138..-14, z -25..-40) of instanced fox fans that jump + throw their arms up with the crowd level, judges' tower with THE BREAK marquee (JIDDA LAGOON PRESENTS · THREE WAVES · ONE HEAT), JOSS at the tower with the heat sheet, five-board lineup in the sand, palms, 3 flamingos, far landmarks from the plan (castle, mountain, town, lighthouse, windmills).
   WAVE: one swell at a time rolls in from z 122 at 4.6 m/s, steepens, BREAKS at z 64 (just inside the yellow TAKE-OFF RING, red PEAK buoy marks the peak), then PEELS down the line at ~9.4 m/s (ramps up, surges ±18%). Whitewater behind the peel, a curling LIP mesh over the first 13 m ahead of it (the tube), shoulder flattens further out. Wave frame: a = down the line (world x = -a), d = in front of the crest (world z = zc - d). All tuning in TUNE at the top of surf-break.js.
   RIDE: lineup = lie on the board, stick paddles around, wave comes → PADDLE · TAP 3 (meter; need 3.1) → pop-up + DROP (LATE DROP if steep). Stick = where to point on the face (right = down the line, up = toward the lip, left = back to the curl); no stick = trims to mid-face. Gravity down the face + wave push vs drag, flat shoulder is heavy so the curl catches up. 1 CARVE = BOTTOM TURN (low) / SNAP or OFF THE LIP (high, climbing) / CUTBACK (stick back, ROUNDHOUSE if it ends in the pocket) / CARVE. 2 TRICK = air: 360 · up RODEO FLIP · down SUPERMAN · sideways GRAB (land before it ends or eat it); on the face HANG TEN. 3 JUMP = big air off the lip when climbing (hitting the lip fast also pops a small air). TUBE = low on the face under the lip near the curl (TUBE x.x S on exit). Points × pocket multiplier (closer to the curl) × speed, repeats are worth less.
   WIPEOUTS: OVER THE FALLS (top of the face at the curl), CAUGHT BY THE WHITEWATER / CLOSED OUT IN THE TUBE, ATE IT ON THE LANDING, PEARLED. Fox flies off, board cartwheels on its leash, washing machine under the foam, pops up gasping; crowd OOOH. CAUGHT INSIDE (sat on the wrong side of the peak) = tumble, not counted. Ride ends: KICK OUT (over the back), RODE IT TO THE SAND, OUTRAN THE WAVE, FLEW OUT THE BACK.
   SCORING: wave score = 10·(1 − e^(−points/1500)), ×0.72 after a wipeout, +0.3 for riding it out; five judges' cards ±0.45, high + low struck, middle three averaged. FREE SURF: 3 waves, best two ≥ 11.00 → FINAL vs JOB: Job first, alternating, 3 waves each; Job rides a choreographed line on the same wave (drop, tube, air 360, off the lip, cutback, rodeo flip, kick out; TAP TO SKIP) and scores from TUNE.JOB (7.4 · 6.7 · 8.3, shuffled ±0.3). Best two wins. Win = save flag surfWon + best (jidda.surf.best.v1). Crowd noise + cheers scale with every move.
   TEST HOOKS: window.__surf.course()._catch() (on a breaking wave), _air(), _trick(k), _wipe(), _cards(), _skipTo('final'), _press(n), _dbg(); __surf._pump(n) steps frames; __surf.setPaused(true) freezes real time for tests.

   ✓ SURF v2 (Ben's answers, session 33; backups backup/jidda/surf-break v1.js + Jidda Surf v1.dc.html):
    AUTO-SURF: the board surfs itself (S-turns through mid-face, pocket held by trimming speed: brakes when > 8 m from the curl, pushes when < 3 m). The stick only AIMS THE MOVE (fighting-game style: direction + button).
    1 = neutral: bottom turn / snap by where you are · ↓ BOTTOM TURN (drops low first) · ↑ FLOATER near the curl (rides the falling lip, immune to over-the-falls) else SNAP / OFF THE LIP · ← CUTBACK · → TUBE STALL (hand drag, slows so the curl catches you).
    2 = in the air: 360 · ↑ RODEO FLIP · ↓ SUPERMAN · ← AIR REVERSE · → KICKFLIP (board flips under him) · on the face: HANG TEN · ↓ CHEATER FIVE · ←/→ BARREL ROLL (pops a small air with a roll).
    3 = paddle in the lineup · GO FOR AIR (climbs to the lip by itself and launches).
    THE 8 TOKENS: gold 8-coins laid on the face down the line on every wave (mid, high, low, ON THE LIP = floater, last one UP IN THE AIR = jump), +60 each, ALL 8 +400; stats card TOKENS n/8.
    VIEW button (yellow, under SHEET + RING; also V): BEACH · LOW (cinematic, near the water) · BEHIND.
    Tube window wider (easier). Target 13.00. Job easier: TUNE.JOB 6.1 · 5.5 · 6.9 (+1.0 on a wave he steals).
    FINAL = Job DROPS IN BEHIND YOU on every one of your 3 waves: gap meter (JOB n M BEHIND YOU, red ON YOUR TAIL under 3 m); he closes at peel speed + JOB_PUSH (+0.25 per wave); every move you land buys room; if he gets past you = JOB STOLE THE WAVE (your ride ends, ×0.85). Touching = BUMP, both wobble, you lose 15% speed. Cards: yours, then Job's for the same wave.
    Scoring retuned: SCORE_K 2600, flow trickle halved, tube = 120 + 150/s.

     PARK (88 × 132 m, sunset, graffiti walls, lamps on every lane, skyline): main lane south→north = 1 cone gates · 2 three gap trenches (fall in = bail, back before that gap) with ledges either side · 3 two yellow rails · 4 curved kicker. East = half-pipe (36 m, 2.2 m walls, steel coping, decks). West = round bowl + access ramp. Coach TONY (arctic white fox, vest, knee pads, clipboard, stopwatch) + chalkboard at the start; speech bubble + radio box.

     RIDING: coasting (board rolls on), gravity on transitions, kick turn when you stall on a wall, pipe auto-straighten, coping = VERT AIR (straight up, turns you round, lands back in), kicker launch, rail magnet (phones), walls you cannot ride up block you.
     TRICKS: 2 in the air: tap = KICKFLIP, hold = deck keeps flipping (DOUBLE KICKFLIP, 360 TRE FLIP with body spin, TRIPLE TRE, THE 8 GATES); let go before you land (0.3 turn grace) or BAIL. Vert tricks ×1.5. Ground: hold 2 = MANUAL, tap 2 = ollie + kickflip. Also GRIND, GAP, AIR, KICKER AIR, BIG AIR. Moves within 1.4 s stack into a COMBO (pot × moves). Bail = lose combo + 50, keep rolling.
     SCHOOL: 6 lessons (+200 each, auto-teleport to the next start, tip after 22 s stuck), time bonus vs 5:00. CONTEST: 60 s, TROPHY_SCORE 2000 → flag skateTrophy + item skateTrophy (Home shelf later). Save: skate.park.<id>.best, flag skateSchool. Demo (7 beats). Synth lo-fi loop until Ben uploads MP3s.

     ORDERS: Meru townsfolk (Kia, Pell, Otto, Marla, Michael Jay, the Fisherman, the Sage, a gate guard, Doc Braun, Flick) with tickets on top (name, patience bar, burger + toppings, fries, drink, price). Stars 1-3 (right + fast); tips; wrong order is refused. 7 recipes unlocked by day (Meru Burger, Garden, Cheeseburger, The Castle, Pickle Back, Double Gate, Meru Melt), fries day 2, shakes day 3. Day goes up when you serve 3 + day. Pay = wage + prices + tips (save.addGold). EMPLOYEE OF THE DAY star (stat meru.burgers.stars). Serve 3 = BURGERS UNIFORM (flag burgerUniform: red cap + apron on Ben). Kitchen upgrades bought on the results card: 4th grill spot, fryer bell, fast pour, patty warmer, diner jukebox (stats meru.burgers.upg.<id>).

Every hand-over cuts to the CUSTOMER'S FACE with a reaction card: THRILLED! (3★ + fast, excited mood + hop + sparkles, big tip) · HAPPY (3★) · NEUTRAL (2★) · UNHAPPY (1★, sad) · INSULTED! (wrong order, angry, refused, bag comes back, patience −6 s); then the change panel. The demo auto-picks.

## DIALOGUE + POP-UP STANDARD (Ben, 3 Oct — every map): STACKING: when a box is up (ambient speech like Monk Fox, a note) AND an "E · Talk to / Enter / Open…" prompt shows, the prompt sits ABOVE that box (one bottom stack, prompt first). In a real conversation (one you started, or a scripted one) all other chatter is silenced (monk sermon + pair chatter muted, captions hidden). Every message is clickable on desktop: prompt = E, toast = dismiss, dialogue = advance. DIALOGUE CONTROLS: footer button NEXT (NEXT · E on desktop) jumps straight to the next line; a red ✕ (white x) top-right closes the conversation and returns you to play, EXCEPT on plot-required dialogue (scripted scenes with a follow-up, comms calls), which have no ✕. Look: every dialogue box, toast, prompt chip, note, callout, tip, sermon, results / transport card, lock pick, jukebox, darts panel = BLACK (#000) panel, 2px YELLOW (#ffd23a) outer border, #3a3836 inner rules, WHITE text, YELLOW titles (speaker names, h2 titles, small labels), roles/hints #cfcac4. Red (#ec3013) stays only for action buttons, the E key chip and meters. Applied: Town Square, Arena, Bridge, Space Dock, Hollow Vale (Tank Range has none).

## THE PLAYER IS NAMED BEN (Ben, 3 Oct): CAST.player.name = 'BEN'; every dialogue speaker plate for the player says Ben (Town Square, Arena, Space Dock), Max calls him COUSIN BEN, Hope's comms use BEN, the Combat Workshop fighter picker reads 'fox: BEN / HOPE / NOBLE', the character switch shows BEN, the Journal shows BEN · 8 GATES. New worlds: take the name from CAST.player.name.

## COMPASS BAR (Ben): a 30 px black bar just UNDER THE TOP BANNER, full width (pad controls stay at the bottom where they were); N/E/S/W yellow; TAP the bar = PLAY WITH MINIMAP on/off; centred on where the fox faces (±90°, yellow centre line + notch); N (red) / E / S / W white, NE/SE/SW/NW grey, ticks every 5°; ENEMIES = red dots, faded when far, solid when near, PULSING faster the closer they are and the more you face them; OTHER FOXES = blue dots (none in the workshop yet); BUILDINGS = white diamonds (grey when far) whose name fades in to solid white as you turn toward them (workshop demo: BARRACKS, ARMORY, GATE are placeholders). Hidden in menus / camera set.

## PLAY WITH MINIMAP (Ben): MENU → MAP has a PLAY WITH MINIMAP button (yellow) → split screen: the MINIMAP fills the TOP half and is the SAME map as MENU → MAP (A-F/1-6 grid, laser + sweet-spot rings, numbered dummies, crates, arrow + view cone, compass, 10 m scale), red ✕ top-right returns to normal play; the PLAY WITH MINIMAP button sits above the map in the MAP tab; the 3D view with the fox is the BOTTOM half with the controls on it, exactly where they are; context messages (DIG HERE / STRIKE! / SMASH IT) show over the minimap; the same button reads EXIT MINIMAP VIEW to go back. Normal play has no band.

## PHONE CONTROLS STANDARD (Ben, 3 Oct — use this layout everywhere: vehicles, fighters, minigames)
Left: fixed JOYSTICK bottom-left (113 px half-transparent ring, white N/E/S/W arrows, 45 px white knob that follows the thumb; drag anywhere on the left half), EYE button (40 px) just right of the joystick, low on the bottom edge: HOLD + drag = look around (sideways + up/down) and it SNAPS BACK the moment you let go; on foot TAP = POV (first person from the fighter's eyes, body hidden, stick still moves you, hold + drag looks around in POV too; tap again = back to 3rd person; eye turns yellow while in POV); in vehicles tap = look back 1.3 s. The CAMERA button (top bar) is the one place to set + lock a lasting angle/zoom. Right: buttons 1 (57 px, bottom edge flush with 2's bottom), 2 (69 px, bottom-right corner), 3 (57 px, above 2, right edge flush with 2's right); small extra (scope/menu) 42 px between them. Two-finger PINCH on the 3D view = zoom (mouse wheel on desktop). No drag-to-look anywhere else, no on-screen text hints. MAP HUD v2: header MERU · TRAINING GROUNDS / COMBAT YARD + GRID cell (A-F × 1-6) + HEADING (N/NE… + degrees); map ±20 m with major/minor grid, column/row labels, yellow frame, centre pad, player = yellow arrow that turns with your heading + view cone + pulse ring, laser-range (dashed red) and sweet-spot (green) rings around you, dummies = numbered red diamonds (grey when down), crates = brown squares, compass N, 10 m scale bar; legend grid YOU · DUMMIES n/n UP · CRATES n · LASER RANGE. Tapping the QUEST tip in the top bar opens JOURNAL → QUESTS (tap again closes). CONTROLS v3 (Ben): ITEM BAG tap = open ITEMS, hold 1 s = use the PINNED item (no dot/badge); middle button tap = WEAPONS (scope flashing: tap = SCOPE, hold 1 s = WEAPONS); top-bar ☰ = JOURNAL, with a row at the very top (above the tabs): BACK TO ORBIT (black, gold space-shuttle icon that every 4.5 s rumbles, lights a little flame, lifts off and fades, then settles back) · OPTIONS (sound, phone controls, camera set, reset camera, slow motion) · HOW TO (was INSTRUCTIONS; phone + keyboard controls) · LOAD · SAVE; menu tab text all white, SCOPE tab far LEFT (SCOPE · ITEMS · WEAPONS · JOURNAL · MAP); every close is a white ✕ in a red square; the SCOPE tab has its own row above the tabs: SCOPE · TIME STOPPED + red ✕ on the right; top bar order HP/EN · CR · WEAPONS (scope icon) · MAP · CAMERA · ☰; PAD WORD LOOP (70 s): each control shows its word for 10 s in turn, then its icon again: 1 MELEE → 2 RANGED → 3 JUMP → middle MENU → eye VIEW → bag ITEM, then 10 s of icons only. ONE 40 s word clock so only one button ever shows words: MENU word 10-20 s, MAP word 30-40 s, CAMERA/ANGLE words 3-6.2 s and 23-26.2 s (1.6 s each), icons the rest; camera icon has no box, shows 8 s then CAMERA 1.6 s, ANGLE 1.6 s. Menu tab labels centred; ITEMS listed as a column (icon · name + effect · count/USE). Menu order top→bottom: (Journal only: ORBIT · SAVE · LOAD) → tabs (SCOPE · ITEMS · WEAPONS · JOURNAL · MAP) → HP/EN + red ✕ → tab content. JOURNAL (new menu tab between WEAPONS and MAP): CHARACTER = three fox cards BEN / HOPE / NOBLE (selected one filled in its colour; each shows LV, HP bar, XP bar; tap = switch fox; Ben's XP from the shared save, Hope/Noble from save.stats.xpParty / hpParty, level n at 50·n·(n+1) XP), ATTRIBUTES per fox (Ben's design, save.stats.attrParty overrides): BEN STR 7 · SPD 5 · SMT 6 · AIM 5 · HRT 8; HOPE STR 4 · SPD 3 · SMT 8 · AIM 3 · HRT 7 (blind); NOBLE STR 5 · SPD 8 (chair tech + arms) · SMT 6 · AIM 8 (upper-body focus) · HRT 7; selected fox's NAME + LV repeat above ATTRIBUTES; fox cards show LV with a small XP bar beside it (no HP); REPUTATION · MERU (save.stats.repMeru, NEWCOMER/KNOWN/TRUSTED/HERO OF MERU), KARMA meter OUTLAW ↔ HERO per fox (Ben from save.stats.karma, Hope + Noble start at +10; save.stats.karmaParty). RULE (Ben): HP and EN shown in the menu header (Journal, Weapons, every tab) and the top bar are ALWAYS the selected fox's; each fox keeps its own HP/EN pool (move-lab POOL, swapped on switch). Fox cards have no hover effect. Fox switch: the shown fox snaps onto the player spot + leaves POV. QUESTS = each quest with kind chip, progress bar, steps with ✓ / next highlighted: TRAINING GROUNDS (tutorial: hit a dummy, 3-hit combo, equip the STAFF, called shot) and MERU (main, the 2D guide steps verbatim, ticked from save flags). ➤ SET COURSE tracks a quest (8gates.trackQuest.v1): the top bar shows COURSE · <quest> with the next step; ✕ UNSET COURSE clears it. In the worlds the quest compass should follow the tracked course. CHARACTER SWITCH (phone pad, 52 px round, north-west of the joystick): fox-head badge: two black ears with white inner ears poke out the top, white top half FOX, thin yellow divider, black bottom half SWAP + three pips (current fox lit yellow), black + white double outline, press shrinks it; tap cycles BEN → HOPE → NOBLE, saved. ITEM BAG (bottom-left, stacked just above the EYE, 44 px; moved up so it never touches button 1 at 390 px): tap = use the HOT ITEM (default HEALTH bar; coloured dot + count badge), hold 0.45 s = open the menu on ITEMS (pauses) with a HOT ITEM picker (HEALTH · PEP · CELL, saved in meru.hotItem.v1). Camera button = video camera in a yellow box, cycles icon → CAMERA → ANGLE every 1.6 s. Time label swaps JANUARY 1 (30 s) ↔ SPEED TIME (10 s) loop, on the shared 40 s clock (tap = +1 hour). CAMERA BUTTON (top bar, between CR and WEAPONS): pauses the game, swaps the joystick + buttons for a SET & LOCK CAMERA sheet (viewfinder brackets, rule-of-thirds grid, PAUSED · CAMERA chip; drag the view to angle it, pinch / wheel to zoom; ZOOM − + and TILT ▼ ▲ bars; presets CLOSE · CLASSIC · WIDE · HIGH; ✕ cancels back; LOCK CAMERA saves distance + tilt to meru.combatTune.v1). TIME BLOCK (clock on top, date line under it, left edges aligned): shows the date (JAN 1, 28-day months), every 3 s swaps to PRESS · SPEED UP; each tap skips ahead exactly ONE HOUR (no auto speed; label shows +1 HOUR / +n HOURS for a moment, block tints, rolls the date at midnight); 12-hour clock with AM/PM beside it; game clock starts 10:00 AM (phone portrait: time block right-aligned so the M of AM lines up with the ☰ glyph above), 1 game minute per second, stops while paused. MERU TOP BAR in the Combat Workshop (Ben: show the player experience): same black bar + yellow rule as the Town Square — HP/EN bars, QUEST · MERU TRAINING GROUNDS 'Train with the dummies', period · weather + clock, CR (shared save gold), WEAPONS (opens the menu's weapons tab), ☰ (opens the menu); portrait = two rows like Meru. The workshop's MENU/PAD/SCOPE tool buttons hide while the phone pad is on. SCOPE v2: no more shudder (follow camera and scope framing no longer fight: follow damping skipped while scoping, shake off); target shown STRAIGHT-ON from the side facing you, its full height fitted to the clear band with ~7% padding top + bottom (height measured from its meshes), camera snaps to each new target; 3D reticle hidden while scoping. SCOPE OVERLAY on the view: vignette + scanlines + a sweeping line, CALLED SHOT card (target, range bar with sweet-spot tick, distance + IN RANGE / SWEET SPOT), yellow corner brackets around the target, spinning dashed ring + red crosshair, HEAD / ARM / BODY / LEGS call-outs on leader lines at the right heights with live hit % and queued ×n (tap = queue that shot). SCOPE IN THE MENU (Combat Workshop): the small scope/menu button (38 px) between 1 and 3 swaps its icon scope ↔ menu every 3 s while it pulses; tapping it in scope mode freezes time AND opens the MENU on a new first tab SCOPE (tabs SCOPE · ITEMS · WEAPONS · MAP): the menu becomes a bottom sheet with the called-shots HUD (target, ◀ ▶, BODY/LEGS/ARM/HEAD, queue, CLEAR/EXECUTE) and the camera closes in on the target in the clear view above it; other tabs still work; closing the menu leaves the scope; EXECUTE closes it and fires. NO AUTO TARGETING (Ben): Combat Workshop starts with lock-on OFF and attacks go where the fox faces (no auto-turn to the nearest dummy, St.autoAim false); the Lock-on toggle and Tab (pick a target) still exist for testing. PHONE CAMERA (on foot, move-lab.js): 25% further out (another 20% in portrait), a little higher, and it leads 2.6-4.6 m ahead of where the fox faces, so the fox sits low on screen with the way ahead in view (not while locked on). Built in Tank Range.dc.html and Combat Workshop.dc.html.

## Rules for every world (see CLAUDE.md)
One connected outdoor map with zones (paths become zones, bracket names kept) + fade interiors + separate battle maps. Off-path wilds with creatures that stay off the path; paths always lined with lamps or trees. Shared save, story/choices, enemy library. Backup before each world. Phone budget.

## Step 1b — BATTLE POLISH (decided 2 Oct, user) — NOT STARTED
Feel: LIGHT AND ARCADEY (quick, forgiving). Work in the Combat Workshop first (move-lab.js: dummies + new enemy types), then roll into the Arena, Caves and Bridge. Scope, in this order:
1. COMBOS + NEW MOVES (one button stays one button):
   - 1 MELEE tapped in rhythm = a 3-hit chain: slash → back-slash → spin finisher (bigger arc + knockback). Each hit opens a short window (~0.35 s) for the next; miss it and the chain resets. Forgiving: buffered input (a press slightly early still counts).
   - HOLD 1 = HEAVY: charge glow (0.4 s), release = overhead smash, breaks guard, small shockwave.
   - 1 IN THE AIR (after 3 JUMP) = air attack: downward plunge, bounce off the enemy.
   - PARRY = tap DODGE just as a hit lands (~0.15 s window): slow-mo flash, the attacker staggers, the next hit is a critical.
   - MIX: 2 RANGE right after a melee chain = 'launcher shot' finisher (laser burst, EN cost).
   - Combo counter + style letter on screen (the Arena's style rank, made shared).
2. HIT FEEL: hit-stop (40-70 ms freeze on contact, longer on finishers), impact flash on the foe, sparks, knockback scaled by move, camera shake (small), layered hit sounds (light / heavy / crit / parry).
3. EN SYSTEM: the laser costs EN per shot, heavy/launcher cost more; EN refills over time and faster on melee hits (rewards mixing). Top bar EN becomes real.
4. ENEMY LIBRARY (engine/enemies.js): melee (circle, telegraph, swing), ranged (keeps distance, aimed shots with a visible charge), charger (wind-up glow, dash, wall-stun), boss pattern sets (phases like Nelly). Telegraphs are big and readable (arcadey).
5. ONE SHARED ENGINE: merge meru-arena.js and engine/combat.js into engine/battle.js used by the Arena, Caves, Bridge, Nelly's boat fight hooks and every later world.
7. THE SCOPE (2D toggleScope, surface_meru.html ~7394-7720): tap the SCOPE icon (top bar WEAPONS row) = the WORLD FREEZES (slow = 0) and a CALLED SHOTS panel opens. Targets = hostiles (and the pulsing attack target) within weapon range ×1.6, nearest first; ◀ ▶ cycles. Pick a zone per shot: BODY (odds 1.00, dmg ×1.0, reliable), LEGS (0.85, ×0.8, halves its speed), ARM (0.70, ×1.2, disarms), HEAD (0.50, ×2.6, critical + reels). Hit chance = base (0.75 + 0.20 in the range sweet spot at 45% of weapon range + smarts/speed skill, max 0.98, 0 if out of range) × zone odds (head capped 0.88). Queue up to THREE shots (EN cost per shot), CLEAR or EXECUTE: the scope closes and a cinematic volley plays at 1/5 speed (camera holds, each round fired in turn, misses go visibly wide, hits spark + damage + the zone effect), then time snaps back. Floating note: 'Queue up to three called shots, then EXECUTE.' Bring into 3D as part of the battle polish (a VATS-style pause), using the top bar's scope button. BUILT IN THE COMBAT WORKSHOP (move-lab.js, 2 Oct): Q / SCOPE button freezes time, yellow reticle on the target (nearest first, ◀ ▶ / arrows cycle), zone buttons 1-4 with live hit %, queue of 3 (EN each), CLEAR / EXECUTE (Enter): volley at 1/5 speed with zone-coloured bolts, misses fly wide, hits pop damage + SLOWED / DISARMED / CRITICAL. Range 22 m, sweet spot 9.9 m. Scope camera: closes in on the target from the player's side, sized so the whole body fits the clear band between the top and the CALLED SHOTS panel (camera.setViewOffset shifts the frame up; distance from the band height + FOV); key hints and bottom buttons hide while scoping; the volley keeps the framing. Still to do: put it in the real fights.
6. HEALTH / DEATH / RESPAWN: shared rules (hurt flash + i-frames, low-HP warning, knocked out → respawn at the last safe spot with a small CR cost or none, heal items from the wheel).

Phone pad + top bar. Debug: __veh.course()._skipTo(n) / _killAll(). TRY ON iPHONE: portrait + landscape, read the board, drive the whole course, the pit waves. v2 (Ben): the in-world blackboard now stands straight ahead of the tank at the start (SGT. TREAD beside it); everything opened up (road 28 m with lamps at ±15.5, cones ±7 every 5 m, pivot ring r 6.5, 12 m ramp, brick walls 26 m wide, targets spread ×1.5, the Pit r 44 with cover/towers/barrels spread ×1.35, map 360 m); robots ~45% bigger (drones 0.75, Sentries/Chargers 1.45, Wardens 1.9 scale; Warden slam ring 8 m); robots arrive EARLIER: 3 drones drop in at BREAK THROUGH, 2 Sentries + 2 drones at TARGETS, the Pit's wave 1 starts on entry, 1.5 s between waves (29 robots, 32 tokens). v3 (Ben): LOOK = EYE BUTTON (44 px, above button 3): drag it any way to ORBIT (sideways = round the tank, up/down = higher/lower; eases back behind the tank 1.5 s after you let go), TAP it = LOOK BACK for 1.3 s (camera swings in front, looking back); PINCH with two fingers on the 3D view (or mouse wheel) = ZOOM 0.55-1.8x, stays where you leave it (api.lookBy(dx, dy), zoomBy(f), lookBack()); the old drag-anywhere look is OFF in the range (lab opts.noDrag; api.lookBy(dx)). ON-SCREEN JOYSTICK: fixed bottom-left (150 px half-transparent ring with N/E/S/W arrows + white knob that follows your thumb); drag anywhere on the left half of the screen. TARGETS swivel to face the tank. STREET LAMPS are breakable (no longer solid): a slow knock BENDS the pole (leans away, light flickers), a fast hit or a second knock snaps it: it falls (TIMBER!), light dies, small blast where the head lands (+25, 25 dmg to robots there). SCRAP DRONES 2.3 scale (tank-sized); ramming at speed hits them for 18 + shoves them back. 4 TURRETS (planted Sentries in sandbag rings beside the road at (-22,4) (22,10) (-26,30) (26,36)) wake as you reach the jump and open fire the moment you land (2-bolt bursts, 3 dmg, red aim lines), so you clear them while the early drones close in (33 robots). TWO in-world blackboards now stand beside the road just outside the hangar (x ±10.5, z -88, angled toward the door), each with the same lesson on BOTH faces + a spinning token on each face; drive up to either to open it; SGT. TREAD stands by the right one; start arrows run straight down the middle. Board button = full-width red ENTER THE COURSE (BACK TO THE COURSE later) + a fun line under it. PHONE PASS: the blackboard has a PORTRAIT version (900x1600 canvas: title, tank drawing, big controls, goal on two lines, coin after GRAB TOKENS) picked when the screen is taller than wide; landscape phones show the board full height with ROLL OUT beside it; shorter stick hint. `Phone Preview.dc.html` shows the range at 390x844 and 844x390 side by side. Scrap drones now 1.15 scale (about half the tank). CONTRAST + KEEP-IN: off-road dirt darker brown, road light concrete with white edge lines + dashed centre line, red/white kerbs along both edges, Pit floor lighter; a red/white striped barrier fence (solid) rings the whole course (x ±68, z -100..174, joined to the hangar front); yellow chevron ARROWS on the ground that pulse in sequence: out of the hangar, both sides round the board, and before every section.
Idea: hangar arrival pad + blackboards (approach = full-screen lesson) + weapons on display (Meru: STAFF) + one fox standing by each area + exit transporter locked until the basics are done → battle area (cover, destructibles, upper decks). Meru vehicles: TANK + SPEEDBOAT. Each vehicle/weapon area is a STAND-ALONE MINIGAME (own page, exportable) that the hub stitches in.

## Session log
- CROSS-VEHICLE PASS (Ben: apply what the later builds taught to the rest, only where it makes sense): (1) GROUND SHADOW BLOB under every ride (vehicle-lab.js updBlob): phones have no shadow maps, so every vehicle now sits on a soft ink blob sized to its footprint (longer for cars), on the ground, ramps or the water surface; fliers (heli, chute, glider, jetpack, balloon…) also get it on desktop above 2 m, shrinking + fading with height so landings and the chute TARGET are easier to judge. (2) ENGINE VOICE (updEngine, SND table): one looping note per motor ride (tank, moto, truck, racecar, boat, jet ski, heli, crane, mech, spider servo, bumper-car / hoverboard electric whine) that climbs with speed, jumps on NITRO, drops when wading, goes silent on pause / hidden tab; human-powered rides stay quiet. (3) HANDS FOLLOW THE BARS: the moto, mountain bike and jet ski riders now push/pull the handlebars with the stick (like the boat's wheel and the racecar). (4) Boat-wake buoy rocking already applies to every water ride. (5) BUOYS REACT TO BLASTS (buoyBlast in boom(), so every explosion counts: tank rockets/cannon, truck fireballs, heli missiles, mech, boat torpedoes): pushed away by distance, popped up out of the water spinning, splash ring on the way down, then the spring brings them home. SAVED FOR LATER (Ben): a lock-on marker for every auto-aim weapon.
- DONE SPEEDBOAT (vehicle 6, in engine/vehicle-fun.js as 'boat', key 6; lives in the lake): deep-V planing hull built from 17 sections (red bottom, ink boot stripe, white topsides, crowned front deck, open cockpit with side decks), black rub rail, chrome bow grab rails, 8 GATES decals + 8 roundels on the topsides, teak floor, red bolsters, bucket seat for the driver + rear bench, console with glowing gauges, steering wheel that turns with the stick, curved windscreen with chrome frame, port red / starboard green nav lights, stern light + 8 pennant that flaps faster with speed, OUTBOARD (red cowl + white cap + vents + 8, leg, plate, spinning 3-blade prop) that steers and trims up on the plane. Sits low in the water at rest, rises and lifts its bow ON THE PLANE, banks into turns, rooster tail from the prop + bow spray. 1 = TORPEDO from alternating bow tubes (runs along the surface with a white wake, homes a little on the nearest target ahead, hits the shore = BEACH LEAP and blows up on the sand: knocks walkers, smashes crates, 40 dmg to cars; ends in the water = KABLOOSH water column), hold 1 = SPREAD of 3 (tube mouths glow red while charged). 2 = POWER TURN (180 spin, spray wall, soaks walkers within 7 m, +40 if fast) / in the air = barrel roll. 3 = NITRO. Kicker = big air. POLISH v2 DONE: WATER-SKIER on a yellow tow rope from a chrome ski pylon (fox in a white/red vest on two skis, handle bar; rope 9 m, verlet physics so he swings wide in turns and carves out side to side; sits chest-deep until the boat is fast, then UP ON SKIS! +10 and +6/s while up, spray at the skis; rides up the kicker = SKIER AIR, lands = SKI JUMP +50; leaves the lake or a fast POWER TURN = SKIER DOWN / WIPEOUT, comes back behind the boat after 2.5 s). Wake ROCKS the buoys (bob + tilt that fades). Debug: window.__sk(). NEXT: 'who drives' + enter/exit vehicles in the worlds.

Moto + truck are marked DRAFT until their passes. Fastest (36 m/s), low camera. 1 = hydraulic BATTERING RAM punch (spiked steel head on a chrome piston out of the nose), hold 1 = RAM CHARGE (ram locked out + burst for 0.9 s, 2.4 s recharge on the button ring); rammed cars get LAUNCHED (fly, spin, bounce, can land FLIPPED), crates smash, walkers BONK. 2 = TWIN LASERS on the sidepods (alternate, auto-aim, hold = auto-fire, 10 dmg), tap 3 = NITRO, hold 3 = handbrake DRIFT (both rear tyres mark + smoke; 0.7 s = DRIFT BOOST, 1.6 s = SUPER DRIFT BOOST). LAP TIMER on the red ring (chequered line, LAP / LAST / BEST + progress bar). Keys 1-4 pick vehicles. - DONE HOPE WEAPONS (all of them): `engine/hope-weapons.js` = HOPE_MELEE (sweep trips → next hit +50% · staff 3-hit, 2nd spins, 3rd thrust · tap 3 taps → floor shock line 8 m · beat = DROP THE BEAT, 120 bpm tick, on-beat streak +25% each up to 3×) / HOPE_RANGED (echo dart homes on last-heard target · tip on a cord, pulls · bell lob, 2.2 m burst, confused 3 s · GUIDE DRONE robot bird rides her shoulder, tap 2 = peck a target 2.5 s) / HOPE_HOLDS (hold 1 ECHOLOCATION: 14 m pulse, enemies glow 5 s through walls, hits on them = CRITICAL ×1.5 · hold 2 WAVE OF SOUND cone, knockback, ARMOR BREAK +25% taken 5 s · hold 3 in air CANE VAULT + landing slam · hold 3 on the ground, stick still = FEEDBACK SHIELD, returns shots) / HOPE_PASSIVES (SIXTH SENSE: SENSE tell 0.45 s before a shot lands, roll on it = PERFECT, no damage · STEADY GROUND · SUNGLASSES — the last two are flags for when enemies get trip/confuse/flash attacks). Hope is the third fighter in the Combat Workshop (FIGHTER PLAYER/HOPE/NOBLE, N cycles; saved). TODO: Cane Workshop preview of her weapons; party AI use on the Bridge/Arena.
- DONE NOBLE WEAPONS (first pass): `engine/noble-weapons.js` = NOBLE_MELEE / NOBLE_RANGED / NOBLE_HOLDS data + nobleWeaponKit(...).attach(noble) (props on the chair: wheel spikes, armrest rams, backrest horn, fold-out rocket pods, hover jets, ground wave; play(key), hover(on), update(dt, speed) → pose {yaw, tilt, lift}; makeShoe(shoe|boot|homing), makeStars(), stunSeconds(userData)). `engine/creature-ai.js`: B.stun(s) — frozen in place (returns idle, moveSign 0) until it wears off. COMBAT WORKSHOP (move-lab.js): Noble is a selectable fighter (MENU → WEAPONS → FIGHTER PLAYER/NOBLE, or N key; saved in meru.combatHero.v1); his MELEE/RANGED tabs list his set; tap 1 = melee, tap 2 = throw (one boot at a time), hold 1 = SPIN-OUT (also smashes crates), hold 2 = LAUNCHERS (2 homing mini-rockets, small blast, 3× energy), hold 3 in the air = HOVER (1.4 s); dummies get STUNNED stars (2 s, medium class). No scope on the chair. JUMP (Noble): 3 = ROCKET JUMP (jets fire, half gravity: slow rise + slow fall, single tap ≈ player's double-jump height, no double jump); in the air tap 3 = CRASH (slams down, damage + radius grow with the height at the tap, popup CRASH x m), hold 3 = HOVER. Melee: presses under ~0.67 s always use the equipped weapon (only SPIKE WHEEL spins); SPIN-OUT needs a deliberate hold. NOBLE WORKSHOP: Weapons panel previews all nine on the chair. TODO: dizzy face + stars in creature-lab when B.stunned; Noble weapons in Meru Bridge/Arena party AI; tune numbers.
- NOBLE CHAIR WEAPONS (decided): hands stay on the wheels. Picks: SPIN-OUT (360 wheelie spin, knocks everyone back) = hold 1; ARMREST LAUNCHERS (twin mini-rockets fold out) = hold 2; HOVER (short glide over gaps) = hold 3 in the air. Buttons same as everyone (1 melee, 2 ranged, 3 jump). Build + test in BOTH the Noble Workshop and the Combat Workshop. Ben's NOBLE WEAPON SET (build exactly):
  MELEE (button 1): 1 SPIKE WHEEL: spikes pop out of the wheels, 360 spin, same damage as the sword. 2 BATTERING RAM: two spikes come forward out of the armrests, the press triggers a short fast charge, +25% damage vs sword, then a recharge moment (25% slower than the spike wheel). 3 AUDIO HORN: 360 stun burst, same range as the sword; stun 3 s small creatures, 2 s medium creatures and fox NPCs, 1 s big creatures, 0.5 s bosses (no damage; size class from creature height / boss flag; add a stun state to the creature brain: frozen in place, dizzy face, stars).
  RANGED (button 2): 1 SHOEMERANG (his existing right boot, flies out and back). 2 BOOTERANG: bigger, stronger, slower boots. 3 HOMING SHOE: seeks the nearest enemy, longer range than the standard shoemerang.
  HOLDS: hold 1 = SPIN-OUT (360 wheelie spin, knockback all round), hold 2 = ARMREST LAUNCHERS (twin mini-rockets fold out), hold 3 in the air = HOVER (short glide over gaps).
  Weapons menu: when Noble is selected, MELEE/RANGED tabs list his set instead of the sword/laser set. Build + test in the Combat Workshop (Noble as a selectable fighter: castKit make('noble') + chair rig) and the Noble Workshop (preview each weapon on the chair). Start in a NEW CHAT: "continue BUILD_PLAN.md: Noble weapons".

Host calls fx.pre before ck.animate and fx.post after. Workshop: entrance plays on every select, RESPAWN button, AI death despawns then respawns, every hurt/stagger/armor-break flashes.

- Creature POLISH: engine/face-kit.js faceKit(THREE,toon) → rbox(w,h,d) rounded box + addFace(parent,{x,y,z,s,eyes,brows,mouth,col}) + setFace(face,state,dt). Faces are added automatically in ck.build (table FACES / ext in creature-kit) to the creatures that lacked expression: golems, gate guards (now get eyes inside the visor), Painted Giant, Shardthrower (mouth), crabs (mouth), Spitter, Husk, Slagback/Ember/Ventmaw/First Cinder (light brows), Ashwalker, Thornback, Sporelings, Scarab, serpents/Wyrm/Belt Serpent (brows), Hive Queen, Deadfall, moles, Attendants (machine brows), Specimen, Siphon (eyes on the boiler), Bolt Thrower, Rust Hulk (angry brows over the headlights), Scrapper, Crusher, worms (eyes), Octopus, Snow King, snowmen, The Fed One, Tall One/Spitter (Ur). Moods by state: idle grumpy + blinks, move determined, wind-up angry (teeth), attack roaring, recover tired/panting, hurt pained squint, stagger dizzy (pupils circle, wavy mouth), armor break shocked, die closed. GATE GUARDS: every box in buildBot is now a rounded box (less boxy). bake keeps face parts separate. ck.build(name,{noFace:true}) skips the face.
- ENEMY LIBRARY brain: engine/creature-ai.js createBrain(g, ck) → { role (boss / charge / ranged / melee from userData), hp, max, range, speed, update(dt, dist, state, st) → next state, moveSign(d), hurt(n, weak, state) → state, strike }. Rules: approach to range (ranged keeps 2.6–5.5 m), cooldown, windup → attack → recover (chargers → stagger); every 3rd hit staggers (bosses 6th); armor break at 50% (bosses + 25%); weak point ×1.6, ×3 while recovering/staggered; committed attacks only interrupted by a weak-point hit; Husk / Attendants / Siphon never flinch. Workshop: TRY THE AI toggle (creature fights the circling fox; HIT / HIT WEAK POINT; readout role, HP, fox HP; respawns 3 s after death). Next: createBrain + bakeCreature when spawning creatures in Gaya.
- Creatures PHONE BAKE (engine/bake.js bakeCreature(THREE, g)): merges each joint's static meshes (and their ink outlines) into one mesh per material; parts referenced from userData (joints, weak, part arrays, hat, lamps) or with userData.home, and textured/transparent/vertex-colour materials, stay separate so animation still works. Workshop: PHONE BAKE toggle (on by default) + readout 'before → after DRAW CALLS · PHONE OK (≤25)'. Use bakeCreature on every creature when it spawns in a world.

Wind-up rips a back slab (grinding sound, slab in hand), attack throws it in an arc to a BLUE DISC; 3 slabs then the core is bare (glows brighter) and it switches to a two-arm overhead SLAM (red ring + shockwave). ARMOR BREAK steps the crust: whole → cracked (crack lines, chips) → broken (shoulder slabs fall off). Die: kneels, then shatters into crystal. Kit gained enter(g,state), dur(g,state), userData.events, reach; lab plays rip/throw/slam/shatter events.
- Creatures: Ben loves the crab. COLOUR RULE: GAYA creatures are shades of PURPLE (Crystal Crab, Woken Crab, all golems, Shardthrowers, Painted Giant base). Other worlds keep their own colours (Sump Crab stays drain green-brown).
- CREATURES (Creature Brief, pasted by Ben): engine/creature-roster.js = every family from the brief (48 entries: world, wave, kind, 2D names as variants, look/fight/weak/tell). engine/creature-kit.js = creatureKit({THREE,toon,M}) build(name) / animate(g,state,t,dt) with userData.joints, userData.weak (+weakMat), calls, height; TELL colours (red melee, orange charge, blue lob, purple hazard, gold weak). Family 1 BUILT: Crystal Crab (Crystal / Woken / Sump), all 9 states. creature-lab.js + Creature Workshop.dc.html: roster by world (BUILT / TO DO, wave), notes card, variants, state buttons, turntable, fox for scale, tells + sound (red ring + double clack on wind-up, gold weak point on recover/stagger), fight loop. Rule from the brief: Ben approves each family before the next. Next: Crystal Golem family. Mesh count ~60 incl. outlines; bake/merge pass later for the 25-draw-call budget.
- Battle polish 3: the small pad circle is CONTEXTUAL: enemy within 30 m (and a ranged weapon) = SCOPE icon that pulses faster the closer (1.6 / 1.05 / 0.75 / 0.45 s), RED inside laser range (22 m); otherwise a SANDWICH. Tap = scope / menu; press-and-hold 0.45 s = menu. MENU (pauses the game; right-side panel, I / Esc on desktop, also from the scope panel's sandwich button): tabs WEAPONS (sub-tabs MELEE · RANGED · WEARABLE) · ITEMS · MAP. ENTER SCOPE button at the top when an enemy is in laser range. Items (x3 each): HEALTH BAR +50 HP, PEP DRINK +30% speed 30 s, ENERGY CELL +50 EN. Wearables (worn by default): LIGHT ARMOR -15% damage taken, LIGHT GAUNTLETS +10% melee, LIGHT BOOTS +10% speed. MAP = top-down yard (you, dummies, crates). In the worlds, items/wearables should come from the shared SAVE.
- Battle polish 2: WEAPONS in the Combat Workshop (move-lab.js MELEE / RANGED tables, WEAPONS panel). Melee (button 1): SWORD; TENNIS RACKET (sword dmg, 2 = 360° spin at 2× swing time, returns enemy shots, two-handed); BOXING GLOVES (4× speed, 0.5 dmg, 0.75 reach, 2 = left punch, no rhythm/finisher, two-handed); SPEAR (1.5 reach, 0.75 dmg, narrower arc); WAR HAMMER (0.5 speed, 1.25 reach, smashes crates). Ranged (button 2, tap fires, cooldown buffers): LASER; STAFF (fireball, 1.25 dmg, half rate, explodes, half dmg within 1.4 m); SCATTER (5 beams x0.5, 0.24 s life = short range, half rate); ICE (0.2 dmg, 0.75 rate, chill slows + icicles stick, FROZEN 5 s at 50% and 20% hp); ROCKET (3x dmg, 0.25 rate, 3 m blast half dmg, 3 s fire patch 3 dmg / 0.5 s incl. the player). Dummies now have 300 HP + bars, fall DOWN and respawn after 2.5 s. Three crates (hammer smashes, respawn 4 s; others CLANK). 'Dummy shoots back' toggle: dummy 4 fires red orbs (8 dmg) for racket returns. Player HP 100 (regen after 2 s). Weapon meshes built in fox P.sword / P.gun groups. Next: roll into the real fights + weapon ownership via the shared save / shops.
- Battle polish 1 (Combat Workshop, move-lab.js): PHONE INPUT = floating stick (left 55%, push full 1.2 s = run, FLICK = roll, toggle) + three round buttons: 1 MELEE (tap slash, HOLD = charged POWER HIT, 0.22 s to start, 0.8 s to full, x1.6-3.0 dmg, full circle above 60%, MAX POWER), 2 RANGE (tap shot, HOLD = POWER SHOT: big gold piercing bolt x2-4 dmg, costs 1-3x EN), 3 JUMP (tap = jump on release, tap again in the air = DOUBLE JUMP x1.12 with a forward flip; HOLD 3 > 0.15 s + push the stick = ROLL that way; keyboard: hold Space + WASD). RHYTHM COMBOS: each swing ends on a beat (tick + gold ring under the fox that shrinks to the feet); next press within ±0.13 s of it = PERFECT x1.3, two perfects into hit 3 = RHYTHM FINISHER x1.82; presses in the first 45% of a swing are ignored (no mashing); off-beat still chains, no bonus. 1-3-1 in rhythm (gaps 0.1-0.95 s, the second 1 in the air) = SKY SPLITTER: dive slam, shockwave ring, 4 m AoE x2.4. Keyboard mirrors it (J/click hold, K/right-click hold, Space double-tap). Workshop: PAD toggle (on by default on touch / under 900 px), sidebar hidden under 900 px. Next: roll these into meru-arena.js + the cave/bridge combat.

- Meru session 24: NELLY + THE BOAT BATTLE (worlds/meru-nelly.js). Toon model: four humps with belly + spikes + spots, flippers, a five-bead tail with a fin (rises for the sweep), an eight-bead neck on a moving curve, head with snout, horns, frills, teeth, opening jaw, eyes with lids/brows (calm / angry / hurt), red hit flash, waterline rings. Fight follows the 2D rules: spawns 10 s after boarding 14-26 m away; first time ever = 'SIGHTING of a SEA MONSTER — get prepared!' + a 3.4 s camera look at her rising (flag nellyIntroDone; the MERU-SEAmonster.mp4 film is not in the project yet); 9 HP, phases at 66/33%; circles at a standoff that tightens; rock volleys 1/3/5 with red landing rings (8 dmg); phase 2+ sinks and lunges at where you were with a visible wake (breach 12 dmg + push); phase 3 rears and tail-sweeps if close (10 dmg + push); backs off to arm's length. Harpoon gun on the bow swivels to aim; 1 MELEE or 2 RANGE fires (auto-aims at her when up), 2 out max, 0.45 s reload, rope drawn, spears stick and ride her, hit = stagger. Beaten: +90 XP, stat seaMonsterKills, water clear for that trip (boarding again brings her back). HP 0 in the boat = capsize, wash up on the jetty healed. Stepping ashore heals. HUD: Health + NELLY bar + phase line in the fight panel. Camera pulls back and frames between you and her, shakes on big hits. Old lake placeholder removed. Music MERU-SEA-BATTLE.mp3 not uploaded yet (no battle music).

- Play (1c): run with stick, a ring shrinks around the ball, press while GREEN (PERFECT <0.075 s, GOOD <0.16 s, else EARLY/LATE = scatter). Stick direction at the hit aims left/right. Gentle auto-run toward the ball when the stick is idle (weaker each round). Landing marker on your side turns red if the ball will be OUT. Serve: 1 toss, 1 again in the green on the power bar.
- Lessons with FAULT (ballfox): HIT, AIM (two glowing boxes), LOB, SMASH, SERVE, RALLY 5. Done → flag gayaTennisLessons unlocks the tournament.
- Tournament: ROUND 1 SLICE (30g) · SEMI-FINAL LOFT (50g) · FINAL ACE (150g + relic goldenRacket, flag gayaOpenWon). One short set, first to 4 games, deuce/ad. Umpire DEUCE calls the score; score also in the HUD quest strip and the 3D scoreboard. Weak shots (not PERFECT) let the opponent attack.
- Arena: blue hard court, net, stands both sides + far end with an instanced crowd (cheers on your points), royal box, light towers, umpire chair, benches, GAYA OPEN boards.
- TESTED by autopilot: all 6 lessons; matches vs all 3 (press-only bot: beats Slice 4-0, loses Loft 1-4 and Ace 0-4 without steering). Phone portrait checked roughly. Camera raised + tilted down (more court, less sky); pop-up messages sit ON the net (engine reports netY). DEMO added: WATCH THE DEMO on the start card (or ?demo=1) plays Slice by autopilot for 2 games with captions + EXIT DEMO, nothing saved (tested). NOT yet: real phone feel, landscape polish, music, merging into a Gaya map.

## KUFA · THE CISTERN CUP (soccer) [kufaStadium] — v1 BUILT
- Files: `Kufa Soccer.dc.html` (Game HUD vehicle="soccer"), `minigames/kufa/soccer-cup.js` (createSoccer), `Kufa Soccer Phone Preview.dc.html`. Game HUD: added soccer (SHOOT / PASS / TACKLE + icons).
- Ben's picks: shootout-style lessons with MASTER FARUQ then a 3-round cup; buttons 1 SHOOT · 2 PASS · 3 TACKLE/SLIDE; ring for shots, power bar for penalties; side-on TV camera; 3 v 3, always Ben (HADI + NASRIN play themselves), automatic keepers (BAHRI for Ben), you keep goal only in penalties; one 3-minute game, draw → penalties (3 each, then sudden death).
- Shooting: the ring shrinks with every dribble touch (0.55 s); GREEN (<0.075 s) = PERFECT, stick up/down = far/near post. 2 with the ball = pass (stick picks who), without = call for it. 3 without = slide tackle (1.1 s cooldown), with = speed burst.
- Penalties: camera behind the ball, stick aims a reticle on the goal, 1 starts the power bar, 1 again in the green. Keeping: camera behind Ben (net hidden), the shooter leans the way he will shoot, hold the stick that way + 3 (too early = he switches sides).
- Lessons: DRIBBLE (3 gates) · PASS (3 to Hadi) · SHOOT (2 goals) · TACKLE (win it 2×) · PENALTY (score 2) · KEEPER (save 2). Flag kufaSoccerLessons.
- Cup: QASIM'S WATER CREW (30g) · GHAZI'S CARAVAN (50g) · FARUQ'S WATCH (150g + relic cisternCup, flag kufaCupWon). Referee strip, score in the quest strip + 3D scoreboard, pop-ups over the far touchline. Demo: WATCH THE DEMO / ?demo=1.
- Stadium: dry-grass pitch, Cistern Head at centre, goals with nets, boards (ball bounces off them, no throw-ins), stone stands both ends + far side with instanced crowd, palms, scoreboard.
- TESTED by autopilot: all 6 lessons; matches (perfect bot 10-0 R1 / 4-2 final; sloppier bot 5-0 / 2-1). NOT yet: real phone feel, tuning after Ben plays, landscape/portrait polish, merging into the Kufa map.
- Soccer phone portrait: pitch turned NORTH–SOUTH (default; Tweak 'phoneField' = North–South / Side-on). Camera behind Ben's own goal looking up the pitch, follows the ball; stick up = toward the goal you attack, stick left/right = across; shot corner + pass aim use the same mapping. Landscape + penalties unchanged.
- Soccer: phone camera 15% closer in play. FIRST-TIME RING (tennis style): when a pass or loose ball runs at Ben, a BLUE ring shrinks around the ball (red = out of reach); press 1 on GREEN = first-time strike (+power), 2 = one-touch pass. The dribble ring (yellow) stays for shots off the dribble.
- Soccer: kits = Ben's Eight all WHITE, every cup opponent RED (keepers keep their own colours, Faruq black in lessons). Softer kick/bounce/tackle sounds (no square-wave click). Camera closer + slightly steeper (landscape side-on and portrait north–south).
- Soccer rings: now drawn flat ON THE GROUND under the ball (below the fox and ball, not on top), thinner, ~50% opacity with a small pulse; Ben's marker ring softened the same way.
- fox-kit.js: new per-fox wag speed `fox.userData.wagMul` (default 1, every other map unchanged). Soccer foxes use 0.4 (tail wags at 40% speed on the start screen and in play).

## LUXOR · FOXY BOXING [luxorFightingPit] (lessons at the BOXING GYM) — v1 BUILT
- Files: `Luxor Boxing.dc.html` (Game HUD vehicle="boxing"), `minigames/luxor/foxy-boxing.js` (createBoxing), `Luxor Boxing Phone Preview.dc.html`. Game HUD: boxing = JAB / UPPER / BLOCK + icons; HUD now also calls game.rangeUp() / jumpUp() when buttons 2 / 3 are released (hold-to-charge / hold-to-block).
- fox-kit.js: new outfit 'boxer' (bare chest + chest fluff, shorts torso[0], waistband torso[1], gloves torso[2]). Everyone in the fight (Ben, rivals, Coach Bruno, the ref) wears it.
- Ben's picks: behind-Ben over-the-shoulder camera; classic arcade (stick only leans left/right or ducks); 1 JAB · 2 tap = uppercut, HOLD = power uppercut · 3 HOLD = block; COUNTER ring on the rival's glove during his wind-up (punch on GREEN = counter, x2 damage + dazed stars); stamina = HUD EN; crowd meter fills on good boxing → 1 + 2 together = 8-GATE HAYMAKER; first to 3 knockdowns (ref counts, announcer + bell).
- Card: PODD (Belly Bump, lean) → VEK (Feint, wait) → SURA (Flurry, block) → FERRO (Iron Hammer, smashes blocks, lean) → LYRA (Spin Uppercut, duck; mixes all tricks). Purses 20/30/40/60/150g; beating Lyra = belt ceremony (Ben raises his arms with the IRON BELT, confetti) + relic ironBelt, flag luxorIronBelt. Lessons (Coach Bruno): JAB · UPPERCUT · BLOCK · DODGE · COUNTER · 8-GATE HAYMAKER, flag luxorBoxLessons. Save keys luxor.box.*.
- Pit: sand arena, raised ring with L mat, ropes + corner pads, 4 tiers with ~200 instanced fans, 6 braziers, IRON BELT on a stand.
- TESTED by autopilot: all 6 lessons pass; fights vs Podd and Lyra won 3–0 by the bot (too easy for a perfect bot; tune after Ben plays). NOT yet: real phone feel, balance, merging into the Luxor map.
- Boxing camera: Tweak 'camera' = Side-on on phones, arcade on desktop (default) / Arcade (low, behind Ben) / High, behind Ben / Side-on TV. Phones (short side < 520px) use the side-on TV view; portrait pulls back so both fighters fit.
- Boxing camera v3 (Ben: 'old-school arcade boxing layout, keep our art'): new default CLASSIC view on every screen. Camera straight behind and above Ben looking at the rival's face; rival drawn a bit bigger (1.04) in the middle of the screen, Ben smaller (0.74) at the bottom and SEE-THROUGH (42%) so he never hides the rival's tells or the counter ring. Older views stay in the Tweak list.
- Boxing v1.1 (Ben): rival 25% bigger (0.95 × 1.25), Ben 14% smaller (0.95 × 0.86) in every view (ceremony full size). Punches more dramatic: long reach, glove swells 45%, body twist, uppercut rises, impact pop at the rival's face + small camera push. Sun moved overhead, Ben no longer receives shadows (the rival's shadow was landing on him). Start card: main button = the regular fight (card no longer locked behind lessons); TUTORIAL · BOXING GYM is its own button.
- Boxing pop-ups (Ben): announcer + pop-up messages now sit ABOVE the rival's head (engine reports headY), bigger type (announcer 17–26px, pop-ups 22–34px). Classic camera aims higher so there is room above his head under the HUD.
- Boxing v1.2 (Ben): 1 = LEFT glove, 2 = RIGHT glove. TAP = jab with that glove, HOLD = charge an uppercut with it (power rises over 0.8 s, then caps), let go to throw. 1 + 2 together (within 0.2 s) when the crowd meter is full = 8-GATE HAYMAKER. Camera swings slightly toward the punching side (≈0.4 m, eases back in 0.4 s; skipped in side-on views). HUD labels LEFT / RIGHT / BLOCK.
- Boxing: rival gloves recoloured so they read against fur and shorts (Vek gold, Sura red, Ferro orange; Podd red, Lyra gold, Bruno red). Vek's black gloves were vanishing into his outlines.
- Boxing contact pass (Ben: 'deep dive, see the glove connect'): every punch now steps in and AIMS the glove at the other fighter's face (head world position), so jabs land on the face and uppercuts on the chin; a dodge makes the rival's glove miss past Ben. Hit spark spawns where the glove is. Guard gloves pushed forward so they sit in front of the face (they were buried inside the big heads; Ferro's Iron Hammer gloves now show above his head). In the classic view Ben's gloves draw on top (no depth test) so you always see them land.
- Boxing: Ben's gloves are now BLUE (#38bdf8, slight glow) so they read against every rival's gloves; jabs land on the cheek beside the guard (left glove → his right cheek, right glove → his left).
- Boxing contact v2: the punching arm is offset in its parent space so the glove centre lands ON the target (measured jab: 0.48 from Podd's head centre = on the face surface; uppercut 0.33 under the head = chin). The puncher still steps in a little. Ben's glove outline hidden in the classic view (it was drawing a dark film over the blue glove).
- Boxing fix: a glove could drift away after a few punches (the aim offset on the arm's x was never reset), so a fighter ended up showing one glove. Both arms now snap back to their own shoulder spot every frame.
- Boxing: on the welcome / end cards both fighters show at the same size (0.95); in the fight the rival stays 25% bigger and Ben 14% smaller.
- START CARD STANDARD (Ben): every sport, job and restaurant start card has 3 buttons in this order: TUTORIAL (top) · the red PLAY button (middle: FIGHT / PLAY ROUND / PUT ME TO WORK) · WATCH THE DEMO (bottom). Sports: TUTORIAL = the lessons, and the cup is no longer locked behind them. Restaurants + Boatworks: TUTORIAL opens the how-it-works card. Applied: Gaya Tennis, Kufa Soccer, Home Hockey, Luxor Boxing, Meru Burgers, Luxor Deli, Jidda Smoothie, Jon Boatworks.
- UNIFORM WELCOME SCREEN (Ben, based on Boxing): new shared child `Welcome Card.dc.html` (kicker, title, text, rows, TUTORIAL · red PLAY · WATCH THE DEMO) shown bottom-left over the LIVE game preview instead of the blackboard. Applied to every vehicle game: Luxor Moto, Nebo Bike Park, Skate Park, Jidda Deep Dive / Submarine / Swim / Jetski / Surf / Inner Tube, Speedboat Bay, Tank Range. TUTORIAL = the game's school/lesson place when it has one (e.g. Moto School), otherwise it opens the saved BOARD (the board is kept and still opens from the in-game BOARD button). Rows = the other places with bests (tap to pick). The sports / jobs / restaurants already use this layout.
- MOTO course pass (Ben: hard to see where you're going / hard to stay on): VOLCANO RUN track now has glowing yellow edge lines, red kerbs, white centre dashes, painted yellow direction arrows and lantern posts on both sides (instanced, phone-light). City avenues got yellow edge lines + lamp posts every 13 m. Stay-on help: in the race, riding past the edge rumbles (slows, dust), gently steers you back along the track and flashes BACK ON THE TRACK; 4 m past the edge a soft wall stops you. In the school, leaving the road adds a light drag.
- MOTO: dashed white centre line like a real road on the Volcano Run (150 dashes, 0.5 × 3.4 m, along the track) and down the middle of all four city avenues (3 m dash / 3 m gap).
- MOTO: chevrons: 36 painted yellow double-chevrons on the Volcano Run surface (bigger) + 28 black/yellow roadside chevron boards on the outside of the bend, facing the rider and pointing the way the track turns.
- NEBO BIKE PARK easier + slower (Ben): ground top speed hard-capped (10 m/s school, 11 m/s contest); a spin/flip that is 60%+ done when you land now counts (SAVED IT!) instead of a wipeout; wipeouts cost 2 s (was 3); bonks keep more speed (0.65×).

Heavy builds (build ≥1.5 + belly ≥0.5) use one sculpted lathe torso (pear belly, rounded pecs, traps flowing into a thick neck, navel, nipples, pec creases), arms set outside the torso, tapered smooth arm/leg joints, sculpted skull (jaw taper, chin, eye sockets, brow ridge, cheekbones), 3D nose + ears, Age slider (wrinkles, smaller eyes, heavy brows), mawashi with hip wrap, front panel, back strap, musubi knot and 17 sagari. Skin uses one shared material with a soft fill so head/body/arms match.

- FILES: `Zion Monster Arena.dc.html` + `minigames/zion/truck-arena.js` (vehicle-lab course plugin, lock 'truck', size 400). Game HUD got truck in VEH_SHORT / VEH_WEAPONS + fireball/oil icons (backup: backup/zion/Game HUD pre-truck-arena.dc.html). ZION_ARENA export = { room 'zionMonsterArena', world 'Zion', entry 'zionMonsterTruckPath', npc 'zionRingmaster' } for the Zion merge (start from Ringmaster VANCE; exits use ?back=).
- BEN'S ANSWERS: all rounds · no tricks, air time only · fireballs always on · cars flatten + bounce the truck · easy, pure fun · reward = trophy relic + truck paint · rival BUGGER.
- ARENA (local coords, bowl x ±66, z ±46): dirt floor, yellow/black barrier walls, stepped stands on 4 sides with an instanced crowd (bounces with the crowd meter; phone 4 rows, desktop 6), 4 floodlight towers, Z + red pennants on the stand tops, FOXTRUCK CRUSH + MONSTER ARENA banners, tyre stacks, crushed-car piles in the corners. CREST MOUND (r 11, 3.2 m, Z crest on top) in the centre. RAMP 1 (x −22, heading N) and RAMP 2 (x +22, heading S), each with 3 two-high CAR STACKS behind it and a landing ramp. CRUSH LINES: west row (x −48), east row (x +48), south row (z −28), 9 cars each. 6 BULLSEYE cars on tyre plinths along the north (z 41). 8 loose cars. PIT (z −45): VANCE (top hat, red coat, speech bubble), the parked CRUSH GOLD truck (the prize paint), BUGGER's green bug truck.
- CARS: one merged vertex-coloured mesh + outline each (phone budget). Crush = flatten to 30%, darken, sparks + glass + dust, crunch; the truck bounces (vy 3.4). Respawn: drop in from 12 m after 9 s (6 s school, 5 s bullseyes in Fireball Alley).
- SCORING: crush 100 · air crush 200 · stack smash 400 · bullseye 250 · torched 120 · air time 300/s · crest air · spin BUGGER out with a fireball 150. Combo banks after 2 s grounded: pot × (1 + 0.25 per extra). Round weights: Crush Lines = crushes full, Big Air = air full, Fireball Alley = fire full (rest ×0.25). CROWD METER fills with banked points → THE CROWD GOES WILD = double points 10 s.
- ROUNDS: TRUCK SCHOOL (1 crush 3 cars · 2 combo ×6 · 3 nitro ramp · 4 stack smash ×2 · 5 bullseye ×3 · 6 crest air) · CRUSH LINES / BIG AIR / FIREBALL ALLEY / FREESTYLE 60 s graded S-D · VS BUGGER 90 s (more cars wins, flag truckBugger) · THE FINAL 90 s (out-score BUGGER: relic foxtruckTrophy + flags truckChamp, truckPaintGold → the player's truck turns gold in the arena). Gold = points/100. Stats zion.truck.<id>.best.
- BUGGER AI: picks the nearest free single car (avoids yours), 9 m/s (8.5 in the final), pauses 1.4 s after each crush, avoids ramps + mound top; fireball = spin out; bumps push. Idle test: ~33 cars / ~4000 pts in 90 s.
- Engine fix in the course: ramp launches gave negative vy (pitch goes nose-down at the lip) → the course sets vy = speed × 0.3 on a ramp takeoff. The Vehicle Workshop yard ramps may have the same issue (not touched).
- TESTED by script: crush rows + combo, ramp 1 with nitro (0.9 s air, stack smashes), crest launch, fireball bullseyes, rival + final to the done card, school lesson 1. NOT yet: real phone check, every lesson by hand, score balance, CRUSH GOLD paint in the Vehicle Workshop.
- v1.1 (Ben): FIRE BARRELS (13 red drums with a flickering flame + hazard band): drive into one = BOOM, truck ARMOUR −25 (shown as the HUD HP bar), combo lost, knocked up; armour 0 = TOWED TO THE PIT (−300 pts outside school, armour refilled). Shoot one = KABOOM +200, chain reaction: blows up barrels within 6 m and cars within 4.5 m, spins BUGGER out. Respawn after 12 s. Truck smokes under 50 armour. BUGGER drives round them.
- v1.1 CARS POLISH: 4 body types (sedan, hatch, pickup with bed, wagon) from extruded side profiles, tinted glass cabin, roof + pillar, chrome bumpers, grille, head + tail lights, side trim, hubcaps, rust patches, odd-colour replacement doors; soft blob shadows; crush = flatten + spread; 2–3 wheels fly off. QUIET POPUPS: course.quietPopups (vehicle-lab popup opt-out) — score text goes to the card's status line instead of floating over the truck. Phone preview: `Zion Monster Arena Phone Preview.dc.html`.
- TRY THIS (truck): crush the whole west row without stopping · nitro into ramp 1 and land on the stacks · full speed over the crest · burn all 6 bullseyes · fill the crowd meter · spin BUGGER out with a fireball · win the final and see the truck turn gold.

## ✓ KYOTO SUMO HALL [kyotoSumoHall] · SUMO STREAK — SPEC (Ben's answers; v1 BUILT, see below)
- PAGE: own page `Kyoto Sumo Hall.dc.html` + `minigames/kyoto/sumo-hall.js`; linked from Kyoto later (?back=). Uses the shared Game HUD + `engine/human-kit.js` (Ben · Big Sumo = player, preset 'bensumo').
- PLAY: real-time brawl. Joystick circles/moves in the ring. 1 PUSH (tsuki shove) · 2 SLAP (harite flurry) · 3 GRAB (belt hold; while held, push = yorikiri carry, 4 = throw) · 4 THROW (nage, needs a grab). Balance/stamina meter per wrestler.
- WIN BY: push out of the ring · throw so he touches the ground · slap-flurry ring-out · lift + carry out (yorikiri). Each move gets a kimarite name popup (OSHIDASHI, UWATENAGE, TSUKIDASHI, YORIKIRI).
- STRUCTURE: one endless bout streak (score attack). Opponents get bigger + tougher each win; one loss ends the streak. Opponents: generic rikishi from human-kit in different mawashi colours, growing in height/build/AI per win.
- DIFFICULTY: easy fun (wide ring edge forgiveness, slow AI early, generous throws).
- REWARD: at 5 wins in one streak → relic `kyotoSumoTrophy` + flag `mawashiSky` (unlocks a SKY BLUE #7dd3fc mawashi for Ben Sumo). Gold per win, best streak stat `kyoto.sumo.streak.best`.
- CEREMONY (all): ring-entry walk under the hanging roof (tsuriyane) · salt throw before each bout · shiko stomp (tap to stomp, crowd cheers) · gyoji referee with a gunbai fan calling HAKKEYOI! to start and NOKOTTA during the bout · crowd throws zabuton cushions on an upset (beating a much bigger opponent).
- HALL: grand tournament hall interior. Raised clay dohyo with straw-bale ring, hanging shrine roof with 4 coloured tassels, tiered crowd on 4 sides (instanced, phone budget), big vertical nobori banners, ring-side judges, lantern light.
- MOBILE FIRST: test 390x844 + 844x390; camera frames both wrestlers side-on; buttons via Game HUD (VEH_SHORT sumo: PUSH · SLAP · GRAB · THROW).
- START: new chat → "continue BUILD_PLAN.md: Kyoto Sumo".

## ✓ KYOTO SUMO HALL v1 BUILT (session after OFFICIAL SUMO)
- FILES: `Kyoto Sumo Hall.dc.html` (page) + `minigames/kyoto/sumo-hall.js` (engine, createSumo) + `Kyoto Sumo Phone Preview.dc.html`. Backup before shared change: backup/kyoto/Game HUD pre-sumo.dc.html.
- GAME HUD (shared): VEH_SHORT/VEH_WEAPONS sumo (PUSH · SLAP · GRAB), icons stomp/shove/harite/belt/nage/drive/release, and NEW hud.vehSwap = [[icon,label]×3] lets a map relabel the 3 round buttons live (sumo: STOMP during shiko, CHARGE before the tachiai, DRIVE · LET GO · THROW while gripping). Other maps unaffected.
- PLAY: stick moves/circles (camera-relative). Free: 1 PUSH (tsuki shove) · 2 SLAP (3-hit harite) · 3 GRAB. Gripping: 1 DRIVE (yorikiri surge) · 2 LET GO · 3 or 4 THROW. Balance = HUD HP, stamina = HUD EN; opponent card shows his BAL/STA. Balance 0 = stumble (any push knocks him down, any throw lands). Walking into him = pushing contest (mass × drive × stamina; belt grip ×1.25). Both brace at the straw. AI tells: push/slap wind-up; a missed push lunges forward (HE MISSED · NOW!).
- WIN BY: out of the ring or knocked/thrown down. Kimarite: OSHIDASHI, TSUKIDASHI, YORIKIRI, UWATENAGE / SHITATENAGE, TSUKITAOSHI, HATAKIKOMI, ISAMIASHI.
- STREAK: endless, one loss ends it. Opponents from human-kit (generic rikishi, own shikona + rank JONOKUCHI→YOKOZUNA, mawashi colours) grow in height/build/mass/AI each win. Gold 10 + 5 × bout (+10 upset). 5 wins in one streak → relic kyotoSumoTrophy + flag mawashiSky (Ben then wears #7dd3fc). Stats kyoto.sumo.streak.best / wins / bouts. Easy fun by default (edge forgiveness for Ben, slower balance loss, throw bonus); tweak "difficulty" = Normal.
- CEREMONY each bout: ring entry down the hanamichi (Ben on the first bout), salt throw, shiko (tap any button, 2 stomps, crowd cheers; auto after 6 s), crouch at the white lines, gyoji HAKKEYOI! → tachiai timing (GREAT / GOOD / SLOW START), NOKOTTA during the bout, gyoji points the gunbai at the winner, zabuton fly on an upset. SKIP button for the ceremony.
- HALL: raised clay dohyo (strata texture), straw-bale ring + square with rope ties, brushed sand, shikiri lines, salt box + water bucket, hanging tsuriyane (gable roof, chigi, katsuogi, purple curtain, 4 tassels), ceiling light rig + light shaft + dust motes, masu-seki boxes with cushions, 7 tiered stands with red seats, kohaku red/white band, 16 nobori banners, lanterns, pillars, 2 kneeling judges. Instanced crowd (36% on phones), blob shadows on phones (no shadow maps).
- Kesho-mawashi apron is hidden during the bout (worn for entry/salt); tweak apronInBout keeps it on.
- POP-UPS (Ben): gyoji call + kimarite/flash sit in one stack ABOVE the wrestlers; the page sends setSafe({top,bottom}) and the camera frames the sumos below that area (portrait: stack under the cards; landscape: one row between the cards; desktop: centred column). Prompt strip at the bottom (portrait above the pad).

- TRY THIS: shiko taps · charge right on HAKKEYOI · walk him back and shove him over · slap flurry at the edge · grab + DRIVE (yorikiri) · throw when his BAL bar is green · sidestep his push · reach 5 for the trophy and sky mawashi.

End: 60%+ = THEY WILL TALK ABOUT IT (gold = house × 1.6, 240 XP, flag kyotoTheatrePlayed), 90%+ also kyotoTheatrePlayedWell; under 60 = A POLITE HOUSE. Stat kyoto.theatre.best. ## ✓ KYOTO SWORD DUEL v1 [kyotoBarracks yard] vs THE SECOND (2D `kendo` rebuilt side-on 2.5D, Street Fighter style)
- FILES: `Kyoto Sword Duel.dc.html` + `minigames/kyoto/sword-duel.js` (createDuel) + `minigames/kyoto/kendo-2d.json` (GAYA.kendo verbatim) + `Kyoto Sword Duel Phone Preview.dc.html`. Game HUD vehicle "duel" (1 HIGH · 2 MID · 3 LOW + icons). Backup: backup/kyoto/Game HUD pre-duel.dc.html.
- Ben's answers: side-on 2.5D, Ben · Sumo in plain dark gi + hakama vs THE SECOND, real katana (toon sparks, no blood), stick + 3 buttons, hold BACK = guard (no 4th button), easy fun, power meter, parry, best of 3, slow-mo final hit, cherry-blossom yard at sunset, + combos and supers.
- PLAY: stick ←→ walk (fwd/back speeds from 2D) · hold BACK = guard (standing guards HIGH + MID, crouch-back guards MID + LOW) · DOWN = crouch. HIGH (men, overhead) / MID (tsuki thrust) / LOW (leg sweep). Hits chain from a landed hit's early recovery = COMBO (counter, 0.88 scaling); HIGH→MID→LOW = SANDAN-GIRI (×1.6 + knockback). Guard just before the hit (within 0.15 s of pressing back) = PARRY (attacker stunned, slow-mo blink, power +30). Chip damage on guard = 2D chip 0.18.
- SUPERS: MASH any attack (4 presses in 0.8 s) = HYAKURETSU HARITE (E. Honda hundred-hand slaps, 10 hits). DOWN → FORWARD + attack = SHIO (a handful of salt flies, DAZES him 1.6 s with stars). Power meter (dealt +18, taken +25, parry +30): full → two attacks at once (or Q / 4) = ISSEN power strike (lunge, breaks guard, 40 dmg). KO = slow-mo + white flash + camera push-in (longer on the deciding round).
- Rounds: best of 3, 75 s (2D seconds), time-out = higher HP %. HP: Ben 160 / Second 120 (2D youHp/hp). AI easy: telegraphed wind-ups, guards sometimes, rare parries; round 2/3 a bit sharper; uses ISSEN when full. Tweak difficulty Normal. Win = 80 g + 200 XP, flag kyotoDuelWon, stat kyoto.duel.wins.
- YARD: barracks facade (plaster + timber, tiled roof, lit windows), bokken rack, nobori (八 門 武), lanterns, 4 sakura trees + falling petals, low sunset sun + long shadows, 2 kneeling samurai watching. SOUND: battle taiko pattern + koto (theatre-audio MOODS.battle), kiai shouts, sword swish, metal clang + ring on parry, slash thuds, slaps, salt hiss, crowd gasp + applause on KO.
- NOT YET: link from Kyoto barracks; the Second's 2D dialogue / reward lines; blade-lock (2D) left out (Ben didn't pick it).
- DUEL v2 POLISH: hit-stop on every hit (longer on ISSEN), victim flashes white, white slash arcs (gold on ISSEN), HP damage-lag bars (red/amber trail drains after 0.5 s), big HITS combo counter on the attacker's side, red glint on the Second's blade during his wind-up (gold on any ISSEN) so attacks are readable, footstep dust + step sounds, dust burst on ISSEN, fighters walk in to their marks each round, ROUND / HAJIME / K.O. slam-in scale, camera pushes in during combos. Backup: backup/kyoto/sword-duel pre-polish.js.
- DUEL v3 · UNARMED + E. HONDA MOVES + JUMP + DISARMS (Ben's picks; this chat). Backup: backup/kyoto/sword-duel pre-unarmed.js.
  · Welcome: two buttons SWORD (vs THE SECOND) / UNARMED (vs TAKANOYAMA, a big rikishi from the sumo streak). Ben unarmed = BEN · SUMO (mawashi). Unarmed moves: 1 PALM (tsuppari thrust), 2 CHOP (overhead chop), 3 KICK (low sumo kick).
  · E. HONDA: mash = HYAKURETSU HARITE (longer in unarmed, palm afterimages, more hits) · hold BACK 0.55 s then FORWARD + attack = SUPER ZUTSUKI (flying headbutt, unarmed) · jump then DOWN + attack = HIP DROP (lands with a shockwave, overhead). Salt still works.
  · JUMP for everyone: stick UP (W). Low cuts miss a jumper; you can't attack mid-air except the hip drop; AI hops now and then.
  · DISARMS in sword mode: ISSEN always knocks the sword out, a PARRY 30% of the time; the sword spins away and lands in the sand; the fighter fights unarmed for 6 s, then SWORD BACK.

FIGHT · VS name / DEMO / BACK. Winning sets flag kyotoDuelBeat_<id> (BEATEN tag in the list) as well as kyotoDuelWon.

Perfect guard on a kunai/shuriken = DEFLECT (flies back and can hit the ninja). Favourites: SHADOW kunai, NIGHT BLUE wall leap, CRIMSON chain. engine: select(you, opp), setRoster, special(), stepCustom(), stepShots().

Order: THE SECOND → TAKANOYAMA → SHADOW → NIGHT BLUE → CRIMSON → RED GENERAL. Each duel best of 3, Ben full HP each fight; foes +7% HP per rung and sharper AI (LV r + rung × 0.6, clamped). Win = 30 + 15 × rung g, 60 + 30 × rung XP → NEXT. Lose = CONTINUE (unlimited, counted) or QUIT. Clear = +300 g + 800 XP, flag kyotoLadderClear, relic kyotoGeneralKabuto, stats kyoto.duel.ladder.clears / .bestCont / .rung (best). RED GENERAL is LOCKED in free select until the ladder is cleared.

WAR CHARGE (red glint wind-up, shoulder rush; guard it or JUMP over → he stumbles open) · HOMURA-GIRI (overhead slam sends a fire wave along the ground: crouch-guard or jump). At 50% HP (gold tick on his bar) THE GENERAL RAGES: red aura + embers, blade glows, bar turns orange, faster wind-ups, double fire wave, super ARMOUR on his wind-ups (hits land but don't interrupt). Boss stage = dusk sky, red fog, red sun. Round 1 call FINAL BATTLE. Engine API: ladderStart/Next/Fight/Retry/Quit, _forceOpp(id); hud.lad, opp.boss/rage.

Tap / E / Space = next line, 1–4 = topic, ✕ / Esc = leave. Talking mouth on the speaker.
