  · DONE · BLADE-LOCK CLASH (2D kendo lock ported; numbers from kendo-2d.json). Backups: backup/kyoto/*pre-lock*.
    TRIGGER: two sword cuts meet (your cut lands while his cut is active or within 0.22 s of striking, or the other way round), both armed, both on the ground, 2.5 s cooldown. The AI sometimes answers your wind-up with a cut of its own to meet it (~1–3 locks a match).
    LOCK (ph 'lock', timer stops): CLASH! + sparks, fighters pressed together with crossed blades, trembling, leaning with the meter; grind sparks + scrape sound; camera pushes in. Meter starts ±0.15 toward whoever struck; every tap +0.19 (lockTap); he pushes back 0.0206/frame after a 14-frame grace (×0.75 Easy fun, ×1.2 General, ×1.4 raging), 130 frames (2.2 s) max.
    MASH: any attack key / 1 2 3 / any phone button / tap anywhere on the screen (full-screen overlay during the lock). Panel under the HP bars: BLADES LOCKED + tug bar (blue Ben / red him, white marker, centre line) + time left, hint MASH 1 · 2 · 3 or TAP FAST · ANYWHERE.
    END (2D calls): meter ≥ 1 → BLADES FREE — YOURS (he takes lockDmg 24 + 0.67 s stun) · ≤ -1 → HE THROWS YOU OFF (SHE for Crimson; 24 × hisPower 1.35, scaled by difficulty) · time up = whoever is ahead, dead even = BROKEN APART (both shoved back, short stun). Demo pilot mashes on its own. Engine: mash(), _lock(), hud.lock.
  · DONE · MOVE LIST + PRACTICE. Backups: backup/kyoto/*pre-practice*.
    Welcome card: header MOVE LIST button; mode row is now 3 cells STORY · LADDER · PRACTICE (label / subline / tag stacked so all fit at 340 px).
    MOVE LIST (modal, pauses a fight): one row per move — name, what it does, input (desktop keys or phone wording). Sword: HIGH CUT · MEN, THRUST · TSUKI, LOW SWEEP, GUARD, PARRY, JUMP, SANDAN-GIRI, HYAKURETSU HARITE, SHIO, ISSEN, BLADE LOCK. Sumo: PALM · TSUPPARI, CHOP, SUMO KICK + the shared ones + SUPER ZUTSUKI, HIP DROP (no blade lock). In practice each move gets a green ✓ once you've done it (n OF 11 TRIED).
    PRACTICE: any YOU + any opponent from the list; no timer (—), no KO (HP floors at 1, both refill 1.6 s after the last hit), your power refills slowly so ISSEN can be tried. Bar under the HP bars: DUMMY (tap to cycle STAND STILL → GUARD → LOW GUARD → JUMP → ATTACK (slow cuts to practise guard/parry) → FIGHT BACK (normal AI)) · MOVES n/11 (opens the list) · RESET (positions + HP) · EXIT. Readout: LAST hit · COMBO damage · BEST combo. Flash/combo/lock labels move down under the bar. Engine: practiceStart/practiceDummy(m?)/practiceReset, hud.prac {dummy, done, cd, last, best}.
  · DONE · KO CINEMATICS. Backups: backup/kyoto/*pre-ko*.
    Every KO (not time-outs / draws) gets a cut: low side angle from behind the winner onto the falling loser, slow-mo, white flash, K.O. call.
    ROUND KO (~2.8 s): cut-in 1.1 s → winner's victory pose shot (camera drifts round him, call cleared) → next round. MATCH KO (~7 s + hold): letterbox bars slide in + HP bars hide → cut-in 1.5 s → REPLAY (red tag): the last 0.8 s before the blow + 0.3 s after, replayed at 0.45× from a high 3/4 angle on the other side, with the impact re-sparked, slash arc + flash → victory pose with VICTORY / DEFEAT (story: HE YIELDS / YOU ARE DOWN), applause or gasp + taiko, lower on screen so it never covers the face → hold: TAP TO CONTINUE (auto 6 s) → end card.
    SKIP: tap anywhere / any button / any key (after 0.35 s) — during cut-in/replay jumps to the pose, then continues. Demo plays it with no replay and a short hold.
    Engine: REC ring buffer (3 s of fighter transforms + poses), S.cine {kind, stage hit/replay/pose/hold}, cineShot(), cineSkip(); hud.cine {kind, stage, bar, canSkip}.

Reusable for other minigames.
    FLOW: welcome card ONLINE 1V1 → Online card: YOUR FIGHTER SWORD/SUMO · CREATE ROOM (4-letter code, no I/L/O) · CODE + JOIN · ?room=ABCD link auto-joins (SHARE LINK = phone share sheet / copy). Waiting → paired (YOU / RIVAL rows, fighter + side, READY tags) → both READY → host sends {k:'start', t0: Date.now()+3000, host, guest, mid} → 3-2-1 → fight. Lowest peer id = host (left, Ben's body); guest = right (sword → THE SECOND body, sumo → TAKANOYAMA). Third player gets MATCH FULL. Done card: REMATCH (both ready again) / LEAVE. Rival leaves or 25 s silence → back to the waiting room.
    NETCODE: host simulates both (opp driven by guest stick, mirrored, + presses through the same press/combo code; equal HP, no AI handicaps, lock = both mash). Host sends a snapshot 20/s (fighter transform + pose, hp/pow/combo, glint/trail flags, phase, timer, calls, flash, lock, cine, loose sword + queued fx: sparks, slash arcs, dust, every SFX/drum call). Guest sends stick on change (≥4/s) + presses; renders 100 ms behind with interpolation; no prediction yet. Tab hidden → {k:'away'} → host freezes the fight; back → 3-2-1. PING shown in the quest strip (CONNECTION WEAK after 3 s silence). Rewards: win +40 g +100 XP, loss +10 g +30 XP, stats kyoto.duel.online.wins/played.
    TESTED: two engines in one page + the real lobby with ?net=local and a second copy in an iframe (pair, pick, ready, countdown, guest walks + hits). NOT tested: real relays / two phones (the build environment can't reach Nostr). Known gaps: guest has ~100 ms + network delay on its own moves (add local prediction if it feels slow), slap palms + salt spray particles don't show on the guest, strict Wi-Fi without TURN won't connect.

    KNOCKDOWNS: ram a rival at speed (>5.5 m/s, head-on-ish, within 1.35 m) → host verdict (2.5 s guard per victim) → victim checks from its own position → falls (no −50) and in TRICK RACE loses 25% (min 50) of its points to the attacker.
    NETCODE: each phone sims itself, sends [x,y,z,yaw,deck,flags,score,lap,cp,-,speed,pitch] 15/s; ghosts damp toward it. Host (lowest id, re-picked if they leave) owns knockdowns and results (ev 'end'). Peers silent 25 s are dropped; everyone gone = race ends.

    TRY THIS (online skate): open the page in 2 tabs with ?net=local · CREATE ROOM in one, type the code in the other · both READY · ram the other skater after landing some tricks · switch to LAP RACE and follow the yellow arch.

## PEARL SAIL TOWER [pearlSailTower] — side-on battle climb (Ben, s35o)
Files: `Pearl Sail Tower.dc.html` (Game HUD + floor card + boss bar + banner + Welcome Card + cleared card; ?embed=1 posts 8gates:minigame-back; ?autostart=1 skips the card) + `worlds/pearl-sail.js` (createPearlSail, Game HUD engine contract; debug window.__pearl._dbg.tp(x, y)).
Ben's picks: Pearl Sail · side-on 2.5D (Metroid) · 6 floors + roof boss · enemies: swooping DRONES, patrolling CRAWLERS, wall TURRETS, ceiling DROPPERS, SHIELDED GUARDS (front blocks, hit from behind) · double jump from the start (the Combat Workshop's: tap 3 in the air, flip) · boss SUDS-9 the rogue window-cleaning robot on its gondola, then the relic · walk in the tower's front door in Meru 2 · player only · the tower's own look (glass offices, overrun by its own security bots).
Combat = Combat Workshop tuning (move-lab.js loadTune: walk, jump 8, gravity 24, swing/windup/reach/combo window/finisher, sword 25, laser 15, fire rate, energy) + its rules: 1 slash chain, on the beat = PERFECT x1.3, 3rd = finisher; 2 laser (stick up / W = aim up, light auto-aim); 3 jump, again in the air = double jump. Down + jump drops through ledges. Coyote time + jump buffer.
Floors (FLOORS in pearl-sail.js; one-way ledges, the slab hole alternates sides, a yellow grate fills it; route check warns in the console if a step > 2.5 m up or > 4.5 m across): 1 LOBBY (revolving door = exit, E) · 2 OPEN OFFICE · 3 SERVER ROOM (solid racks) · 4 EXECUTIVE SUITE · 5 ATRIUM (pendant lights) · 6 MAINTENANCE · ROOF. Checkpoint = first landing on each new floor; KO = respawn there with full HP. Bots drop pearls (+12 HP). Items heal from the bag.
SUDS-9 (900 HP): SPRAY (lobbed soap bubbles, shootable) · SWEEP (gondola docks, red strip = JUMP over the squeegee; phase 3 adds a yellow HIGH sweep = STAY DOWN) · then DOCKED 2.3 s = WEAK POINT (full damage; otherwise the dome takes 40 % shots / 60 % sword) · phase 2+ releases drones. You can stand on the gondola deck. Win: relic 'pearlSail', flag pearlSailCleared, +150 CR first time (+25 after), best time stat pearlSailBest.
Meru 2 entry: L.BUILDINGS 'pearlSail' (open, hiring.play → no NOW HIRING board; the prompt says BATTLE · PEARL SAIL) at the landmark's revolving door (383, 222.6).
s35p (Ben: could not reach floor 2, no hint): every ledge step is now 1.6 m (was up to 2.4, needed a perfect apex double jump), route check warns above 2.0; a pulsing yellow ARROW floats over the next ledge up (then the ceiling grate); lobby has no turret; hurt knockback 4 (was 7). s35q FIX (verifier): the top ledges sat under the solid slab (1 m head room → shoved into the hole column, fell). Now per floor: 1.6 → 3.2 → 4.8 (that one reaches into the hole column) → 6.4 INSIDE the hole column → grate 8; the X push ignores head overlaps < 0.3 m; route check warns on head room. DEMO: Welcome Card WATCH THE DEMO = autopilot (climbs with the same jump / double jump, slashes + shoots bots, jumps the boss sweeps, hits SUDS-9 when docked, takes the relic; no damage; 6 s with no new height = hops to the next ledge); yellow DEMO PLAYING chip = take over. s35r ENEMIES + GRAPHICS (backup backup/pearl-sail/pearl-sail.pre-polish.js): every bot has a glowing eye, flashes white when hit, gets knocked back, shows a small HP bar for 2.5 s, and dies with a hit-stop, a spin / flip arc, chunks that bounce, then a glow pop. CRAWLERS crouch (eye flickers, '!') then LUNGE 3 m. DRONES + TURRETS show a red aiming laser while they wind up (dodge it). GUARDS: shield flashes on a block; after a bash they STAGGER 1 s with the shield down ('OPEN!' = hit the front). Graphics: real 3D city outside the glass in 3 depth rows (parallax as you climb), fog, light shafts per floor, floor-tinted light (server room blue, executive warm), slash arc (gold on the finisher), laser glow + muzzle flash, double-jump ring, vignette. NOT done: iPhone 390×844 / 844×390 check; sound (all muted); boss music; my other picks (floor transitions, secrets per floor, more SUDS-9 phases).

### PEARL SAIL · 3D FLOOR TEST (s35s, Ben picked option 2 "3D on rails")
`Pearl Sail 3D Floor.dc.html` + `worlds/pearl-floor3d.js` (createPearlFloor3D; window.__pearl3d._dbg.tp(x, z)). Floor 2 OPEN OFFICE as a 48 × 36 m 3D floor: elevator lobby → cubicle farm (6 pods of 1.3 m partitions you can jump over, desks, monitors, knocked-over chairs) → print room → meeting corridor with 3 glass side rooms (loot: pearls + credit chips, 10 CR each) → break room → red-lit stair EXIT. Camera rides BEHIND + ABOVE along the ROUTE polyline (blends round corners); stick / WASD move relative to it; tall walls between camera and fox fade to 18 %. Yellow dashed floor strip + an arrow 6 m ahead of your best progress. 15 bots with 3D brains (crawler crouch + lunge, drone aim line + swoop, turret laser sight, guard shield front ±70° + stagger). Combat = side-on rules + auto-aim (laser cone 0.55 rad / 18 m, slash turns to the nearest bot within 4.5 m). Shadows on desktop. KO = back 3 m along the route. FLOORS3D data (walls, route, foes, loot, gates, powers, exit): Floor 1 OPEN OFFICE → STAIRS → Floor 2 SERVER ROOM (rack aisles with LED fronts, glass DATA CORE room with a glowing core, cable trays, cooling units; dark floor). GATE SPAWNERS (little torii: dark pillars, gold lintel, swirling red portal, red beam above the walls; 160 HP, always-on HP bar, spawn a crawler / drone every 6 s while you are within 22 m, max 2 each): floor 1 has 3, floor 2 has 4, all OFF the runner; the stair door has a red laser barrier until every gate is down ('DESTROY THE GATES · N LEFT'); near the end with gates up the arrow points to the nearest gate; killing a gate kills its spawns. POWER-UPS off the runner (12 s): SPEED BOOTS ×1.5, TRIPLE LASER (3-way), POWER SWORD (×2, bigger swing), SHIELD (blocks 3 hits, blue bubble). Card: floor, bots, GATES n/total, power + time. Fade between floors; start of a floor = 2 s safe + HP ≥ 60. Floor 2's exit = ROOF → cleared card. Backup backup/pearl-sail/pearl-floor3d.pre-floors.js. NOT done: floors 3–6 + roof boss in 3D; phone check; demo for the 3D version.

Sword = 1 hit (power sword 2, finisher +1), laser = 1. Hit = wobble + white flash, ≤ half HP darkens. Break = collider removed (cut a way through anything), debris.

- SCORE: per floor SPOTLESS (0 broken) +100 CR / CLEAN (≤ 10 % broken) +50 CR (banner on the next floor). Card shows DAMAGES n CR · n BROKEN live. Cleared card: REPUTATION by % broken (CAREFUL GUEST < 3 · HANDY < 12 · MESSY < 30 · WRECKING BALL < 60 · OFFICE TORNADO), clean bonus, props broken, damages bill, insurance covers 500 CR, charged by Pearl Sail Holdings (taken from gold after rewards).

### ✓ PEARL SAIL · ROOF DUEL (s36) — the finale of Pearl Sail 3D: BEN (cast fox) vs SUDS-9 on the roof, in the Kyoto Sword Duel engine
Ben's answers (s36): ALWAYS BEN (no fox select; Noble / Hope not built) · ONE long fight, 3 phases, no timer · SUNSET · SUDS-9 dark grey like the 3D bots · win = 200 g + 300 XP + PEARL OF THE SAIL relic · loss = RETRY right away · finisher = he falls off the roof with his gondola toward Meru.
Backups: backup/kyoto/sword-duel pre-foxes.js, backup/kyoto/Kyoto Sword Duel pre-foxes.dc.html, backup/pearl-sail/pearl-floor3d.pre-duel.js, backup/pearl-sail/Pearl Sail 3D Floor.pre-duel.dc.html.
FILES
- minigames/kyoto/duel-foxes.js (new): foxFighter() = Ben from engine/cast.js + the POSE ADAPTER (the duel's human IK targets → fox arm / leg aims, lean pivoting at the hips, head; fake human L FOX_L; the fox sword is lifted off the arm and held in the sword paw with P.blade like the katana; the gun stays on the near arm, gunTip marker). makeSuds9() = the robot (dark grey #4a4e55 / #1f2226, box head, angry slanted red eyes pulsing deep red → red, brows, mouth grille, red band, SUDS-9 plate, blue soap tank, yellow beacon, 2.6 m steel squeegee pole on the camera side, soap nozzle arm, stocky legs) + sudsPoseOf(f, S) + upd() (drives the roof car / gondola rig and the JUMP / DUCK warning strips).
- minigames/kyoto/duel-pearl-roof.js (new): STG.pearlRoof — roof deck, helipad H under the fighters, hazard stripes + red line, parapet + glass rail, stair housing with the ROOF door + PEARL SAIL sign (left end), HVAC units, the PEARL SAIL sail mast with the glowing pearl + two pennants in the wind (right end), the window-cleaning ROOF CAR on its rail with jib, cables and GONDOLA (follows SUDS-9 along the edge), the tower's glass facade under the edge, MERU far below (one instanced mesh of towers in Meru 2 colours with lit windows, every rooftop below eye level; landmarks: Lake + Crest towers, casino + pink neon, bank columns, castle on its hill, arena ring; river, sea), low sun over the sea, warm clouds, drifting soap bubbles, haze fog, front fill light. Camera far 1400 only in pearl mode.
- minigames/kyoto/sword-duel.js: opts.pearl branch (modules lazy-loaded only then; Kyoto unchanged — checked: menu, best of 3, timer, demo, no errors). Kyoto Sword Duel.dc.html: ?pearl=1 (&embed=1, &back=…) → pearl card, pearl result card, MOVE LIST for the fox, quest strip THE ROOF · SUDS-9 · PHASE n OF 3, boss bar ticks at 60 / 30 %.
- worlds/pearl-floor3d.js + Pearl Sail 3D Floor.dc.html: the atrium ROOF door → "THE ROOF · SUDS-9" + fade → full-screen panel iframe (Kyoto Sword Duel.dc.html?pearl=1&embed=1&fox=ben); the tower sleeps (no render) while it is open; message {type:'8gates:pearl-duel', won, hp, time} → finishDuel(won): win → cleared card "The Pearl Sail is clear" + rows ROOF BOSS · SUDS-9 BEATEN + Relic PEARL OF THE SAIL; loss (BACK TO THE ATRIUM) → back 3.6 m inside the ROOF door, "WALK IN TO TRY AGAIN". ?duel=1 = atrium, gates down, straight into the duel. Welcome card row "The roof".
PLAY (Ben fox): 1 HIGH · 2 MID · 3 LOW, guard / parry / jump / crouch, SANDAN-GIRI, ISSEN (full power), BLADE LOCK (squeegee vs sword) as in Kyoto · DOWN + 2 (or down, forward + hit) = LASER (3 bolts from the gun tip, 6 dmg each, 2.2 s cooldown, pops bubbles) · mash = FOX FLURRY (the hundred-hand slaps rebuilt as quick cuts) · no salt / headbutt. Fox can't be disarmed.
SUDS-9 (280 HP Easy / 320 Normal — up from the handoff's 230/260 because it's one fight, not best of 3; can't be disarmed, less knockback): rises from below the edge on the gondola, "PLEASE HOLD STILL FOR CLEANING." · LOW SWEEP (red striped strip on the floor + JUMP!: jump it, or crouch-guard) · HIGH SWEEP (yellow bar at head height + DUCK!: crouch under it, or stand-guard) · perfect guard on a sweep = PARRY · after a sweep he usually DOCKS (backs onto the parapet by the gondola): dazed 2.3 s = WEAK POINT, hits ×1.5 · SOAP SPRAY: 3 / 4 / 5 lobbed bubbles (guardable, slash or laser them; one that lands leaves a slippery SOAP PUDDLE for 6 s) · squeegee chop / jab / low poke + POWER WASH (MVB boss moves) · PHASE 2 at 60 % "RINSE CYCLE" (faster sweeps, double sweeps low→high, more spray) · PHASE 3 at 30 % "SPIN CYCLE" (red aura, armour on wind-ups, fastest).
KO: K.O. hit cut → replay → FALL shot (camera out past the edge: he slumps into the gondola, the cables snap, he tumbles down the glass facade toward Meru, "SUDS-9 · OFFLINE") → VICTORY pose → result card. Win: first time +200 g +300 XP + relic pearlOfTheSail + flag pearlSuds9Beaten; later wins +25 g +50 XP; stats pearl.duel.wins / .best (time) / .losses. Result: CONTINUE (embedded → back to the tower) / FIGHT AGAIN; loss: RETRY / BACK TO THE ATRIUM.
DEMO button = autopilot (jumps the low sweep, ducks the high one, lasers from range, rushes the weak point).
TESTED (headless): full autopilot fight through both phases, KO → replay → fall → victory → result + save, loss card, tower → panel → loss → back at the door → walk in again → win → cleared card, Kyoto regression, 390×844 + 844×390 (touch HUD: stick + HIGH / MID / LOW, cards fit). NOT tested: a real iPhone (frame rate), sound balance (still the Kyoto taiko / koto music on the roof).
NOT DONE / IDEAS: NOBLE + HOPE as duelists (Ben chose Ben only; the adapter is ready for castKit().make('hope'|'noble')) · the 3 foxes in the normal Kyoto roster · SUDS-9 drone minions · own roof music · BEN fox in the Kyoto ladder.
- s36b (Ben): NO LASER in this duel (gun hidden, the near paw is a free fist). Buttons for the fox: 1 SWORD (big two-handed overhead: high wind-up behind the head, lunge step, big white arc, screen shake; DOWN + 1 = low crouching cut) · 2 PUNCH (quick paw jab, any guard blocks) · 3 KICK (front kick, shoves him back; DOWN + 3 = TRIP SWEEP, trips him = 1 s stumble; in the air = AIR KICK, flying kick, standing guard blocks). FOX FLURRY only on 5 fast punches (within 1.1 s): five clear hits (paw jab / sword-pommel strike) each with a flash, burst, ring and the foe jolting, then a big gold finishing slash (FOX FLURRY · FINISH!). FOX COMBO = punch → kick → sword on hits (×1.6). Phone buttons relabelled SWORD / PUNCH / KICK through the HUD's vehSwap (Game HUD unchanged). SUDS-9 HIT REACTION on every hit: white flash, torso + head whip back, eyes flare; trip = stumbling pose. Fox sword 1.0 scale (was 0.85). Backups: backup/kyoto/*pre-kick*.
- s36c CARTOON BRAWL (Ben: "make it fun, emphasise the moves with the little arms and legs — take the lead"). Backups: backup/kyoto/*pre-brawl*. duel-foxes.js foxCartoonOf(f, S) = an un-blended CARTOON LAYER on top of the pose (hop, spin, squash + stretch, tail whip, ear flop): HOP PUNCH (2): every punch is a little hop; three in a row within 0.7 s = JAB · HOOK · big UPPERCUT (hops ~1 m, head spin, 11 dmg). SPIN KICK (3): full 360° body spin with a tail whip. TRIP SWEEP: spinning low sweep. DRILL KICK (3 in the air): drills round its own axis. SWORD (1): jumping spin slash, lands with a squash. LOW CUT: half-spin. FLURRY: speed-bag hops on every hit, then a leaping spin into the gold finishing slash. Squash before / stretch on impact, wobble on landing and when hit. NEW HEAD BONK: jump close + 1 → lands on SUDS-9's head, hammers it twice with the sword pommel (7 each, his head squashes), back-flips off; invulnerable during it; camera rises to keep him in frame. COMIC HIT WORDS (starburst sprites: POW / WHAM / BAM / UPPERCUT / THWACK / TRIP / KA-POW / SLASH / KA-BOOM / BONK / FINISH; CLANG on a guard; WHACK / OOF / SPLOOSH when SUDS-9 hits Ben) + camera punch-in on every hit, white speed streaks off paws and feet. SUDS-9: bolts / nuts / screws / panels fly off on hits and bounce on the deck, soap squirts from his tank, head spins all the way round on big hits, PHASE DAMAGE: phase 2 = SUDS-9 plate hanging, loose wire, sparks off the shoulder; phase 3 = cracked eye + smoke from the head. Demo pilot uses the HEAD BONK.
- s36d SPECIALS (Ben: "like Street Fighter Ryu"): FOX BEAM = DOWN → FORWARD + PUNCH (stick motion within 0.7 s; hadouken-style: paws pulled back to the hip with a cyan glow, then thrust forward; a big glowing cyan energy beam with a long tail flies across the lane at 10 m/s, 14 dmg, any guard blocks it, pops bubbles on the way, one on screen, 1.4 s cooldown, comic FOX BEAM!). WHIRLWIND KICK = DOWN → BACK + KICK (tatsumaki: lifts off, spins forward with one leg out, white + gold whirl trails off the foot, up to 3 hits THWACK · WHAP · WHIRL-WHAM, last one knocks him back, standing guard blocks, 1.1 s cooldown). Stick history now also records BACK / DOWN-BACK. Move list, card rows, key strip updated; demo pilot uses both. Backups: backup/kyoto/*pre-supers*.

## ONLINE · UP TO 4 PLAYERS (s37, Ben) — Pearl Sail 3D first
Ben's answers: gamepads for split-screen (max 4 on one screen, auto side-by-side on wide / top-bottom on phones), mixing local + online YES, robots SHARED (host runs them), blaster = SAME TARGETS race (one shared camera on one screen, own crosshair + RELOAD each), boss = everyone duels on their own screen, spectate a friend's duel only after you finish, KO = respawn nearby, anyone in the same world meets automatically + private codes, any fox per player (duplicates OK, colour-tinted). Order: online in the Pearl tower (DONE below) → split-screen → Meru 2.
DONE (this chat): `engine/world-net.js` connectWorld({world, onJoin, onLeave, onState, onEvent, onStatus}) from Ben's fg8 kit (Trystero / Nostr, appId 'foxgate8-worlds', room 'fg8-pearl' or 'fg8-pearl-<code>' via ?room=, ?net=local tabs, ?net=off). pearl-floor3d.js "ONLINE" section:
- SEATS by join time, first 4 play (5th = "This world is full" card: PRIVATE ROOM / PLAY SOLO). Seat 1 = HOST. Slot colours RED / BLUE / GOLD / GREEN (remote foxes: CAST.player with the slot colour on the armour, a coloured ring + name tag, glide damp 10 / snap > 8 m, dropped after 25 s silent; a peer that talks before saying hello gets a 'who').
- My state 10×/s on change + 1 s heartbeat (wall clock): [x, y, z, yaw, speed, air, floor, flags 1 exit · 2 duel · 4 blaster · 8 KO].
- HOST runs every robot / gate / boss: 'world' snapshot 8×/s [nid, type, var, x, y, z, yaw, hp, alive, state, max]; placed robots + gates get nid = order on the floor, spawned ones 1000+. CLIENTS draw them (netFoe: glide + poses + death) and send 'hit' {n, d, fx, fz}; the host applies it (guards still block from the front). The host's robots chase the NEAREST fox on the floor (brain runs with P swapped to that fox; hurt() → 'hurt' to that player). Enemy bolts are broadcast ('eb') so every player dodges their own copy. Broken props broadcast ('pb').
- EXITS WAIT FOR EVERYONE on the floor: standing in the open door = "WAITING FOR YOUR FRIENDS AT THE STAIRS", the host sends 'next' when every seated player on that floor is in → everyone climbs.
- BLASTER RACE: the first player into the break room opens it for all ('blopen'), START starts all ('blstart'); the HOST spawns every target + power-up and sends them ('bs' / 'bp'); a kill / pickup is broadcast ('bk' / 'bpt') so it vanishes for everyone; points go to whoever shot it; friends' live scores in the top bar + on the result card.
- BOSS: drone ambush + SUDS-9 are host-run; when anyone triggers the 75 % call, 'duelcall' gives EVERY player the challenge; each accepts and duels SUDS-9 in their own panel.
- HUD: top-right ONLINE card (status · n/4 FOXES · YOU <COLOUR> · INVITE = share / copy a ?room=CODE link, first tap moves you to that private room) + a row per friend (FLOOR n / AT THE STAIRS / BLASTER · score / IN THE DUEL / KNOCKED OUT).
TESTED (one machine, ?net=local, a second copy in an iframe): seating + host, remote fox appears + follows, host robots on the client, client hit / kill reaches the host and comes back. NOT tested: two real phones over relays, the stairs wait, the blaster race, the boss call with 2 players (the preview throttles background frames so long two-copy tests stalled).
NEXT: (1) test on 2 real phones / 2 tabs (?net=local); (2) LOCAL SPLIT-SCREEN with gamepads (viewports per player in one renderer, P per player, Gamepad API mapping 1 slash · 2 laser · A jump, blaster = one shared view with 1P / 2P / 3P / 4P crosshairs + RELOAD in each player's corner); (3) fox pick per player (Ben / Noble / Hope via castKit + loadCastRigs, tint by slot); (4) spectate a friend's duel after yours (stream the duel snapshot like the Kyoto online 1v1); (5) MERU 2: split up + explore freely, every minigame offers MORE PLAYERS and waits for friends to walk up and take a seat.
- s37 MERU 2 ONLINE (Ben: "do the same for Meru 2"): `worlds/meru2-net.js` createMeru2Net (room 'fg8-meru2' / ?room=CODE, same seats + colours + remote foxes + 25 s drop as the tower), wired into meru2-walk.js (walker.net, walker.netHud(), ticked every frame; foxes only show when you're both outdoors or both inside the same building). Players SPLIT UP and explore freely, nobody waits. Online card top-right (status · n/4 · YOU colour · INVITE share link with ?walk=1&room=CODE · a row per friend: n M AWAY / INSIDE <building> / PLAYING <game>). MINIGAMES WITH FRIENDS: starting a NOW HIRING / PLAY spot broadcasts it ('mg'); friends see the prompt "<NAME> IS PLAYING · TAKE SEAT n" + JOIN when they walk up to the same spot; every seat opens the minigame with the SAME ?room=CODE (+ &players=n), so minigames with online rooms (Kyoto duel, Skate Park) put you together; the others still play solo side by side until each gets its own online mode. Backups: backup/meru2/*pre-online*. TESTED: 2 copies on one machine (?net=local): seats, remote foxes, the shared seat code. NOT tested: real phones, each minigame's own multiplayer.
